ARTICLE 06 门诺弟兄会信仰宣言 · 中英双语对照典藏本

第 06 条：教会的本质 (Nature of the Church)

Article 6: Nature of the Church

信条条文 一、信仰宣言条文
Confession of Faith The Confession of Faith
🇨🇳 中文信条条文
🇬🇧 Original Confession Text

蒙上帝呼召（Called by God）

我们信教会是上帝藉着耶稣基督所呼召出来的子民。凡以信心回应呼召的人，藉着洗礼的公开认信与地方堂会联合。教会成员在圣灵的赋能下，立志在作门徒与见证的生命中跟从基督。

Called by God

We believe the church is the people called by God through Jesus Christ. People who respond in faith are united with the local congregation by the public confession of baptism. Church members commit themselves to follow Christ in a life of discipleship and witness as empowered by the Holy Spirit.

基督的身体（Body of Christ）

教会是由来自各邦国、各种族、各阶层的男女信徒所组成的一体。这身体的元首是基督。教会在同一位圣灵里合而为一，在世人面前彰显基督。教会既以地方信徒群体的形式存在，也作为普世信仰共同体而存在。

Body of Christ

The church is one body of believers, male and female, from every nation, race, and class. The head of this body is Christ. The church, united by the one Spirit, makes Christ visible in the world. The church exists as local bodies of believers and as a worldwide community of faith.

敬拜（Worship）

当上帝的子民定期聚集归荣耀与上帝时，教会便得着滋养与更新。初代教会于七日的第一日聚集，庆祝耶稣基督从死里复活。敬拜的共同体称颂上帝的信实与恩典，重新确立对上帝的忠诚，建立基督身体的各肢体，并在生活与使命中寻求上帝的旨意。教会在施行洗礼与主餐时，宣告救恩的福音。\*

Worship

The church is nourished and renewed as God’s people gather regularly to glorify God. The early church gathered on the first day of the week to celebrate the resurrection of Jesus Christ from the dead. The worshipping community celebrates God’s faithfulness and grace, reaffirms its faithfulness to God, builds up the members of the body, and seeks God’s will for its life and mission. As the church observes baptism and the Lord’s Supper, it proclaims the good news of salvation.*

团契与彼此守望（Fellowship and Accountability）

教会是一个圣约共同体，成员在信仰与生活的事上彼此负责、互相守望。他们彼此相爱、关怀、代祷，分担彼此的喜乐与重担，互相劝诫与规正。他们在有需要时分享物质资源。地方堂会效法新约的榜样，在关乎共同见证与使命的事宜上，寻求更广泛教会的指导与建议。各堂会在爱心、彼此顺服和相互依存的精神中同工合作。

新约圣经指引着具有救赎性质的教会纪律之施行。教会对持续陷在罪中的成员负有劝戒规正的责任。对于悔改之人，堂会予以饶恕并接纳恢复其交通；但对于藐视并拒绝纪律的人，则依规予以正式开除会籍。

Fellowship and Accountability

The church is a covenant community in which members are mutually accountable in matters of faith and life. They love, care, and pray for each other, share each other’s joys and burdens, admonish and correct one another. They share material resources as there is need. Local congregations follow the New Testament example by seeking the counsel of the wider church on matters that affect its common witness and mission. Congregations work together in a spirit of love, mutual submission, and interdependence. The New Testament guides the practice of redemptive church discipline. The church is responsible to correct members who continue to sin. Congregations forgive and restore those who repent, but formally exclude those who disregard discipline.

事奉的恩赐（Gifts for Ministry）

上帝藉着圣灵赐给每一位成员不同的恩赐，叫全身体得益处。这些恩赐应当用于事奉上帝，以建立教会并在世界中服侍。

上帝呼召特定的人来装备教会投入事奉。领袖应当在个人、家庭和教会生活中作效法基督的榜样。教会当以祷告的心慎重辨识领袖，并在爱中予以印证、支持和劝勉归正。

Gifts for Ministry

Through the Holy Spirit, God gives gifts to each member for the well-being of the whole body. These gifts are to be exercised in God’s service to build up the church and to minister in the world.

God calls people to equip the church for ministry. Leaders are to model Christ in their personal, family, and church life. The church is to discern leaders prayerfully, and to affirm, support, and correct them in a spirit of love.

📖 经文引证 (和合本 CUV)

马太福音 16:13-20；18:15-20；约翰福音 13:1-20；17:1-26；使徒行传 1:8；2:1-4，37-47；11:1-18；15:1-35；罗马书 12:3-8；哥林多前书 5:1-8；12–14章；哥林多后书 2:5-11；加拉太书 3:26-28；6:1-5；以弗所书 1:18-23；2:11-22；4:4-6，11-16；帖撒罗尼迦前书 5:22-23；提摩太前书 3:1-7；提多书 1:7-9；彼得前书 2:9-12；5:1-4。 \ 洗脚礼是教会敬拜的一种形式，它能有力地提醒信徒关于谦卑、爱心的服侍与个人的洁净，而这些特质正应当体现在教会肢体彼此的关系之中。*

📖 Scripture References

Matthew 16:13-20; 18:15-20; John 13:1-20; 17:1-26; Acts 1:8; 2:1-4, 37-47; 11:1-18; 15:1-35; Romans 12:3-8; 1 Corinthians 5:1-8; 12-14; 2 Corinthians 2:5-11; Galatians 3:26-28; 6:1-5; Ephesians 1:18-23; 2:11-22; 4:4-6, 11-16; 1 Thessalonians 5:22-23; 1 Timothy 3:1-7; Titus 1:7-9; 1 Peter 2:9-12; 5:1-4. * One form of the church’s worship is the practice of footwashing which can be a meaning- ful reminder of the humility, loving service, and personal cleansing that is to characterize the relationship of members within the church.

神学注释 二、神学研读注释
Theological Commentary Commentary
🇨🇳 中文神学注释
🇬🇧 English Commentary

新约圣经运用了许多不同的意象与文字图画来定义教会。这些意象可以根据相似的主旨和主题归纳为几个群组。

The New Testament defines the church with many different images and word pictures. These images can be clustered into groups with similar motifs and themes.

对教会的描绘（Descriptions of the Church）

第一组意象将教会描绘为上帝的子民（the people of God）。教会是蒙上帝呼召的子民，这里的重点在于上帝：是上帝亲自造就了一批子民（彼前 2:9-10；罗 9:25-26）。新约中的上帝子民包含了所有信徒，无论是犹太人还是外邦人。这群子民取代了地上的圣殿，成为上帝同在与居住的所在（林前 6:19；3:16-17；弗 2:21）。

第二组意象将教会描绘为一个团契共同体（community）。在新约圣经中，“圣徒”（saints）、“忠心的人”（faithful ones）和“义人”（righteous ones）等词汇几乎总是以复数形式出现的。这些词汇所描绘的是教会的整体本质，而非单个基督徒的个体生命。因此，教会是一个圣徒共同体，是被上帝分别出来归属于祂的“圣洁子民”。教会也被描绘为“门徒”、“门道（这道）”（the way）、“奴仆/仆人”、“朋友”、“见证人”、“上帝家里的人/眷属”（household）。基督徒则被描绘为同一家庭中的“儿女”或“弟兄”与“姐妹”。所有这些意象的核心要义在于：教会是一个被视为有机整体的多数人群体。在新约圣经中，整体始终优先于个体，并且整体赋予了个体身份与认同；这与现代西方世界以个体为中心、个体优先于群体的思维截然相反。

教会不仅是一个团契共同体。第三组意象以宇宙性与末世性的视野来刻画教会。教会是“新造的人/新造之物”（林后 5:17）、“初熟的果子”（罗 16:5；林前 16:15；雅 1:18）、“新人/新人类”（弗 2:11-17；西 3:10）、“上帝的安息”（来 4:1-11）以及“光”（太 5:14-16；约 12:35；林后 4:6；帖前 5:5；彼前 2:9）。教会是上帝应许的应验。教会的崇高与宏大，以至于圣经必须借用宇宙维度的意象来加以描绘。这些意象断言：上帝正在教会中行一件新事，上帝正在使万物更新。教会正是上帝在起初创造中所定心意的终极成全。

第四组意象将教会描绘为基督的身体（the body of Christ）。教会是一个由许多不同肢体连结在一起所组成的活泼身体。每一个肢体对于其他肢体以及整个身体的成长都是不可或缺的。基督是这个身体的元首。当各肢体与元首建立正确合宜的联结、并且肢体彼此相助时，教会便得着增长。这一意象凸显了教会在基督里的合一，以及在同一个身体内恩赐的多样性。

Descriptions of the Church

One cluster of images describes the church as the people of God. The church is the people called by God. The accent is on God. God creates a people (1 Peter 2:9-10; Romans 9:25-26). The people of God in the New Testament includes all believers, both Jew and Gentile. This people replaces the temple as the place where God dwells (1 Corinthians 6:19; 3:16-17; Ephesians 2:21). A second cluster of images describes the church as a community. Terms like “saints,” “faithful ones,” and “righteous ones” are always plural in the New Testament. Such terms describe the nature of the church rather than the individual Christian life. Thus the church is a saintly community, a “holy people” set apart by and for God. The church is described as “disciples,” “the way,” “slaves,” “friends,” “witnesses,” a “household.” Christians are described as “children” or as “brothers” and “sisters” in a common family. The point of all these images is that the church is a plurality of people viewed as a collective whole. And in the New Testament, the whole is always prior to the individual and gives identity to the individual, rather than the reverse as in the western world.

The church is more than a community. A third set of images pictures the church in cosmic and eschatological terms. It is the “new creation” (2 Corinthians 5:17), the “first fruits” (Romans 16:5; 1 Corinthians 16:15; James 1:18), the “new humanity” (Ephesians 2:11-17; Colossians 3:10), “God’s Sabbath” (Hebrews 4:1-11), and “light” (Matthew 5:14-16; John 12:35; 2 Corinthians 4:6; 1 Thessalonians 5:5; 1 Peter 2:9). The church is the fulfillment of God’s promises. The church is so grand that cosmic images are used to describe the church. These images assert that God is doing a new thing in the church; God is making all things new. The church is to be the fulfillment of what God intended in the original creation. A fourth set of images pictures the church as the body of Christ. The church is a living body composed of many different members united together. Each member is necessary to the others and to the growth of the whole. Christ is the head of this body. The church grows as different members are properly related to the head and to each other. Such imagery highlights the unity of the church in Christ and the diversity of gifts within the one body.

上帝的子民（The People of God）

描绘教会的各种意象均表明：上帝正在藉着基督与圣灵造就一群子民。这群子民在世界中是一个“另类社会”或“反差社会”（contrast-society / alternative society）。它在其群体生活与见证中彰显出上帝的本性。虽然上帝也藉由其他方式启示祂自己，但教会是上帝临在世界的主要所在，也是上帝在世上成就其宣教使命的首要器皿。

本信仰宣言第6条突出了若干核心主题，强调我们渴望作为上帝的子民一同成长。我们对合一的深切关注，源自“上帝的家”和“基督的身体”等意象，以及哥林多前书1–4章、加拉太书3章和以弗所书2章的教导。合一的主题教导我们：在教会中以及在基督里，原本不同的人如何成为一体。

在再洗礼派和门诺弟兄会传统中，“基督的身体”一直是最受重视的教会意象。信徒不仅与基督联合，也彼此联合形成一个身体。教会作为基督的身体，也是基督向世人所作的公开见证。

在圣约共同体中“彼此守望（互相关顾与劝勉问责）”的主题，反映了对成为一个家庭、上帝家里的人、一群子民、一个身体、一位新妇之真谛的理解，并且顺从了圣经中劝勉信徒彼此负责、互相守望的明确教导（太 18:15及以下；罗 12:3及以下；加 6:1及以下）。

The People of God

The various images describing the church assert that God is creating a people through Christ and the Spirit. This people is an alternative or contrast-society in the world. It reflects the nature of God in its corporate life and witness. Though God reveals Himself in other ways, the church is the primary place of God’s presence in the world and the primary means of God’s mission in the world.

Article 6 of the confession highlights several themes emphasizing our desire to grow together as God’s people. Our concern for unity is drawn from images such as the family of God and the body of Christ, and from teachings in 1 Corinthians 1-4, Galatians 3, and Ephesians 2. The unity theme teaches the oneness of different people in the church and in Christ.

The body of Christ has been a favourite image for the church among Anabaptists and Mennonite Brethren. Believers are united both to Christ and to each other to form one body. The church as the body of Christ is also the public witness of Christ to the world.

The theme of accountability in the covenant community reflects an understanding of what it means to be a family, a household, a people, a body, a bride, and follows explicit teachings that exhort believers to be accountable to each other (Matthew 18:15f.; Romans 12:3f.; Galatians 6:1f.).

教会中的敬拜（Worship in the Church）

初代教会定期聚集敬拜（太 18:20；徒 4:31；11:26；13:44；15:6, 30；林前 5:4；11:17, 18, 20, 23；14:23, 26；雅 2:2）。使徒们极力劝勉信徒不可停止聚会（来 10:25）。教会聚集，是为了称颂上帝的恩典与美善，造就信徒，并成为合一的身体（林前 11；14章）。敬拜的要素包括宣讲圣言、教导、洗礼、团契相交、擘饼、祷告、诗歌赞美、奉献以及共同辨识与决议（徒 2:42；林前 11；14章；弗 5:19；西 3:16）。

在部分门诺弟兄会教会中所实行的敬拜仪式之一是洗脚礼（footwashing）。这一实践基于约翰福音13:1-20中耶稣的榜样和教导。当耶稣为门徒洗脚时，祂放下了权力的特权，树立了真正仆人式服事的典范。基督徒效法基督的榜样，“洗圣徒的脚”，并藉着招待客旅、爱心服侍等诸多行动展现基督的舍己之爱（提前 5:10）。

Worship in the Church

The early church gathered regularly for worship (Matthew 18:20; Acts 4:31; 11:26; 13:44; 15:6, 30; 1 Corinthians 5:4; 11:17, 18, 20, 23; 14:23, 26; James 2:2). The apostles encouraged such meeting together (Hebrews 10:25). The church gathers to celebrate the grace and goodness of God, to build up the believers, and to become a unified body (1 Corinthians 11; 14). The components of worship include proclamation, teaching, baptism, fellowship, breaking bread, prayer, singing, offerings, and decision making (Acts 2:42; 1 Corinthians 11; 14; Ephesians 5:19; Colossians 3:16).

One form of worship used in some Mennonite Brethren churches is the practice of footwashing. This practice is based on Jesus’ example and teaching in John 13:1-20. When Jesus washed the feet of the disciples, he set aside the privileges of power and modelled true servanthood. Christians follow the example of Christ by “washing the feet of the saints” and by many other acts of hospitality, service, and love (1 Timothy 5:10).

教会纪律（Discipline in the Church）

耶稣吩咐教会应当成为一个施行纪律的共同体（太 18:15-25）。“捆绑与释放”意味着就“何为忠心跟从基督”进行伦理道德上的辨识，并随后依据这一共识守望全体成员：劝离那些违背教会共识且拒不悔改的人，同时坚固释放那些持守忠心的人。纪律具有属灵辨识、牧养关怀以及促使个人与整座教会得着更新的功用（加 6:1-2；弗 4:25；林前 5:3-5；林后 2:5-11）。教会纪律的目标始终是将人挽回并恢复其与基督及教会的和好关系。

施行纪律的典范始于“用爱心说诚实话”（弗 4:15），即直接与犯错的信徒进行一对一的坦诚交通。如果对方毫无转变，则由少数几位教会肢体一同参与当面劝诫。若这一步仍不见效，此事才交由全会众共同处理。凡真心悔改的人，教会当予以饶恕，并在其做出必要改变的过程中给予牧养扶持。若犯错之人无视教会的劝勉仍执意陷在罪中，教会便需裁定恰当的纪律处分。圣经教导施行此类纪律，既是为了保护教会内部生命的圣洁与纯全，也是为了维护教会在世人面前见证的正直与公信力。

尽管全体信徒对教会纪律都负有守望责任（太 18:15-25；加 6:1-2），但教会领袖在提供指导与执行纪律方面承担着特别的职责（徒 20:28-31；多 1:5-11；彼前 5:1-4；来 13:17）。领袖同样要向教会负责。若领袖出现错误教导或不当操行，同样必须接受纪律惩戒。然而，鉴于闲言碎语和无端指控的危险，对领袖的控告与纪律处理必须经过审慎的查验（提前 5:19）。

教会纪律的目的包含两个层面：

  1. 挽回犯罪的弟兄或姐妹（太 18:15及以下；加 6:1-2；林后 2:5-11）。新约圣经中明确列出足以导致开除会籍（excommunication，革除教籍）的情形极为有限，仅包括：否认耶稣是道成肉身降世（约壹 4:1-6）、持续沉溺于淫乱之罪（林前 5:1-13），以及背离使徒教训在教会中结党纷争（罗 16:17-18）。
  2. 维护教会在世人面前见证的真实与正直（太 18章）。如果教会无法在每个时代清楚辨识何为对基督的忠心，它就会失去其宣教见证的力量。

在当今教会中，纪律的施行变得十分困难，其原因有三：首先，在门诺弟兄会的历史中，曾存在律法主义式地误用乃至滥用纪律的现象，引发了信徒对纪律实践的反弹与抵触。其次，当代西方社会的高度流动性、个人主义与相对主义，使得教会既难以在“何为罪恶行为”上达成共识，也难以要求人们依照这种共识彼此负责受审视。第三，法律诉讼的威胁使得教会在执行纪律时裹足不前、顾虑重重。然而，这些现实困难绝不能成为废弃教会纪律的借口。纪律对于教会的规正、更新、牧养关怀、基督徒的造就与成长，以及向外的宣教见证而言，都是不可或缺的。

Discipline in the Church

Jesus instructed the church to be a disciplining community (Matthew 18:1525). “To bind and to loose” means to engage in ethical discernment about what it means to be faithful, and then to hold people to that discernment by dismissing those who have violated the consensus of the church and liberating those who have not. Discipline is a function of discernment, pastoral care, and renewal of individuals and the entire church (Galatians 6:1-2; Ephesians 4:25; 1 Corinthians 5:3-5; 2 Corinthians 2:5-11). The goal of discipline is always restoration to Christ and the church.

The pattern for discipline begins with “speaking the truth in love” (Ephesians 4:15) in direct, one-to-one conversation with the erring believer. If there is no change, a small circle of additional church members becomes involved in the confrontation. The matter becomes a concern for the entire congregation if this step is ineffective. The person who repents is to be forgiven and nurtured in making the necessary changes. If the erring person continues in sin against the admonition of the church, the church determines the appropriate discipline. Such discipline is taught in the Scriptures both to protect the purity of the internal life of the church and the integrity of the church’s witness in the world.

While all believers are responsible for discipline in the church (Matthew 18:15-25; Galatians 6:1-2), church leaders have a special responsibility to give guidance and to carry out discipline (Acts 20:28-31; Titus 1:5-11; 1 Peter 5:1-4; Hebrews 13:17). Leaders are accountable to the church. They must be disciplined as well for incorrect teaching or misconduct. Such discipline, however, must be carefully tested because of the danger of gossip and unjust accusations (1 Timothy 5:19).

The purpose of church discipline is twofold. The first purpose is the restoration of the sinning brother or sister (Matthew 18:15f.; Galatians 6:1-2; 2 Corinthians 2:5-11). The New Testament identifies a very limited number of things that justify excommunication: denying that Jesus has come in the flesh (1 John 4:1-6), persisting in sexual immorality (1 Corinthians 5:1-13), and causing division in the church by opposing apostolic teaching (Romans 16:1718). The second goal of discipline is to uphold the integrity of the church’s witness in the world (Matthew 18). The church loses its missionary witness when it does not discern the meaning of faithfulness to Christ in every age. The practice of discipline in the church has become difficult for three reasons. First, the legalistic misuse and even abuse of discipline in Mennonite Brethren history has caused a reaction against the practice. Second, the mobility, individualism, and relativism of contemporary western society have made it difficult both to reach consensus on what constitutes sinful behaviour, and to hold people accountable to such a consensus. Third, the threat of litigation has made churches cautious in the practice of discipline. But none of these problems justifies the abandonment of church discipline. Discipline is necessary in the church for correction, renewal, pastoral care, Christian nurture and growth, and missionary witness.

教会中的事奉（Ministry in the Church）

新约圣经教导，全体信徒都藉着圣灵得着了恩赐，用以在教会和世界中事奉（林前 12–14章；弗 4:11-16；罗 12:3-8）。这些恩赐的运用是为了造就并坚固整座教会。

新约圣经同时也教导，教会需要领袖（腓 1:1；弗 4:11；提前 3章）。教会领导职分的目的，是藉由教导、规正、勉励与爱心的服侍，成全并统筹调配各样不同的恩赐。教会领袖应当是成熟的基督徒，在个人、家庭和教会生活中树立效法基督的品行典范（提前 3:1-13；多 1:5-9）。

新约圣经描述了一种由执事（deacons）和长老（elders，后者亦被称为监督 bishops 或牧师 pastors）组成的两重职分事奉模式。然而，圣经并未将这种模式定为教会绝对规范性的固定制度。新约圣经并没有规定某种特定的教会组织架构。圣经所明确教导的是：凡事都要规规矩矩地按着次序行，其终极目的是为了造就全教会（林前 14章）。至于这种组织次序的具体体制形态，在教会历史长河中以及在门诺弟兄会自身的发展历程中，向来是各有差异、不断调适的。

Ministry in the Church

The New Testament teaches that all believers have received gifts by the Spirit for ministry in the church and in the world (1 Corinthians 12-14; Ephesians 4:11-16; Romans 12:3-8). These gifts are to be exercised for the nurture and strengthening of the entire church.

The New Testament also teaches that the church needs leaders (Philippians 1:1; Ephesians 4:11; 1 Timothy 3). The purpose of church leadership is to enable and administrate the diversity of gifts through teaching, correction, encouragement, and loving service. Church leaders are to be mature Christians who model Christlike conduct in their personal, family, and church life (1 Timothy 3:1-13; Titus 1:5-9).

The New Testament describes a twofold pattern of ministry involving deacons and elders (the latter also called bishops or pastors). But this pattern is not taught as normative for the church. The New Testament does not prescribe a specific form of church organization. What it does teach is that the church should do all things decently and in order for the purpose of building up the whole church (1 Corinthians 14). The precise form of that ordering has varied in the history of the church and among Mennonite Brethren.

教牧应用 三、教牧实践应用
Pastoral Application Pastoral Application
🇨🇳 中文教牧应用
🇬🇧 English Pastoral Application

“教会的本质”这一条目是我们《信仰宣言》的核心基石之一。因为门诺弟兄会与其他再洗礼派群体一样，同属于信徒教会传统（Believers Church tradition），因此我们关于教会的论述对于界定自身身份特质至关重要。再洗礼派在十六世纪的诞生，正是出于对教会本质的深切关切；而三百年后门诺弟兄会的成立，也是出于完全相同的缘由。我们自认属于“信徒教会”，这一特质使我们有别于那些实行婴儿洗礼的传统，并将我们归入这样一群耶稣的跟随者之列：他们坚信，成为教会的一员必须基于个人对主耶稣基督的彻底降服与奉献。

教会在新约圣经的著述中占据着核心地位。当耶稣宣告祂要建立祂的教会时，祂所设立的乃是上帝定意在世上成就其工作的主要建制。基督的教会正是上帝在世上成就救赎的特选使者与代理机构。

如同许多宗派一样，门诺弟兄会当前也正面临深刻挑战：既要持守圣经关于教会本质的根本原则，又要在后基督教的文化处境中保持切合现实的影响力。本信仰宣言第6条正是对我们教会观的系统总结。

The article on the “Nature of the Church” is one of the key elements in our Confession of Faith. Because Mennonite Brethren are, with other Anabaptist groups, part of the Believers Church tradition, what we say about the church is especially important in defining our identity. The Anabaptists came into existence in the sixteenth century because of concerns about the church, and the Mennonite Brethren Church was formed 300 years later for similar reasons. Our self-description as a Believers Church distinguishes us from those who practice infant baptism and puts us in the company of followers of Jesus who insist that being part of the church requires a personal surrender to the Lord.

The church plays a central role in the writings of the New Testament. When Jesus said that he would build his church, he was establishing the primary institution through which God has chosen to do His work in this world. The church of Christ is God’s agent of redemption in the world. Like many denominations, Mennonite Brethren are grappling with the issues of maintaining biblical principles regarding the nature of the church while being relevant in our post-Christian context. Article 6 summarizes our understanding of the church.

会籍与委身（Membership and Commitment）

教会不能自行挑选上帝要呼召谁加入其会籍。凡公开认信因信基督得救、顺服祂受洗的吩咐、并甘心顺服本《信仰宣言》的人，我们均接纳他们加入教会会籍。我们确信，加入教会成为正式成员，是委身于圣约关系（covenant relationship）的重要体现。这项圣约在最基本的层面上是一项庄严宣告：“这是我的属灵子民，我是这个信徒大家庭中的一员。”

尽管教会是在世界中事奉，新约圣经却呼召信徒活出与世人有别的生命。这种“分别”（separation）乃是植根于我们对圣洁的理解。教会必须树立并彰显圣洁的品格，这立即使得教会与世俗世界产生鲜明的分别。向着上帝分别归圣，乃是作为教会的本质内涵之一。当上帝的子民定期聚集彼此服侍并敬拜上帝时，这种圣洁品格便得以培育和展现。（参见第1条教牧应用中关于敬拜的论述。）

Membership and Commitment

The church does not choose whom God will call into its membership. We receive people into church membership who confess salvation through faith in Christ, obey his command to be baptized, and submit themselves to our Confession of Faith. We believe that joining the church as a member is an important expression of a commitment to a covenant relationship. At the minimum, this covenant is a declaration that “these are my people, and I am a part of this family of believers.”

Though the church ministers in the world, the New Testament calls believers to be different than the world. This kind of separation is based on our understanding of holiness. The church needs to model and display the character of holiness, which immediately marks a distinction from the world. Separation to God and to holiness is part of what it means to be the church. This develops as God’s people gather regularly to minister to each other and worship God. (See portions on worship in the Article 1 pastoral application.)

种族主义与和好（Racism and Reconciliation）

在我们的时代，种族仇恨与撕裂是教会必须正视的严峻议题。政府和教育界为实现种族和解所作的种种努力，均未能产生显著成效。其根源在于：这些举措无法触及人的内心深处——而那正是种族主义问题的根源所在。然而，基督的教会带着盼望与和好的福音信息，确实能够带来翻转性的改变。教会在全地之上或许是唯一能够在自身成员结构中展现出跨越种族与文化鸿沟之能力的群体。在北美，几乎每一个社区都居住着不同族裔的人口并深受其影响。负责诠释本宣言的教牧人员必须严肃面对这一课题，恒切祷告、忠心教导，并亲身彰显福音叫人和好的大能。如果教会不主动迎面应对种族撕裂、并不在自身群体中展现出另一种颠覆性的生活方式，我们的福音信息就绝不可能被世人所信服。

Racism and Reconciliation

Racial hatred and division are serious issues for the church to address in our time. Various governmental and educational attempts at racial reconciliation have not produced significant results. The reason for this is these programs cannot touch the heart of a person, the root of the problem of racism. But the church of Christ, with its message of hope and reconciliation, can indeed make a difference. The church is perhaps the only body on this earth that can demonstrate in its own membership the ability to cross racial and cultural lines. Almost every community in North America is being inhabited and impacted by people of different races. The pastoral interpreter of the confession must contend seriously with this matter, seeking to pray for, teach, and demonstrate the reconciling power of the gospel. If the church does not move to confront racial division and demonstrate an alternative, our message cannot be taken seriously.

与其他基督徒群体的合作（Cooperation with Other Christian Groups）

门诺弟兄会主张与其他基督徒群体开展合作。我们长期支持各福音派和跨宗派机构，例如加拿大福音派团契（Evangelical Fellowship of Canada）和美国全国福音派协会（National Association of Evangelicals）。我们乐意与这些机构同工。

然而，更为棘手的问题是：对于那些自称是基督徒、其政治或社会议题的主张令我们赞同、但其神学立场却令我们深感不安的群体，我们当如何与之合作？例如，我们是否应当为了反对堕胎，而与那些基督观与我们截然不同的人联手？我们是否应当为了打击社区犯罪，而与那些神学立场在我们看来存疑的人结成同盟？这些问题无法给出一刀切的通用答案，而必须成为地方门诺弟兄会教会在扎根社区、确立自身见证认同的过程中，需要承担的辨识责任。

Cooperation with Other Christian Groups

Mennonite Brethren believe in cooperating with other Christian groups. We have supported various evangelical and inter-denominational bodies such as the Evangelical Fellowship of Canada and the National Association of Evangelicals. We cooperate with such groups willingly. More difficult, however, is the matter of cooperating with those who are Christian and whose political or social agendas we endorse, but whose theology makes us uncomfortable. Should we join forces to oppose abortion, for example, with those who do not view Christ as we do? Should we fight crime in our neighbourhoods by entering into alliances with those whose theology is questionable to us? These are matters that cannot be answered in a general way, but become part of the responsibility and discernment of the local Mennonite Brethren church as it establishes an identity in its community.

圣灵的洗与属灵恩赐的运用（Baptism in the Spirit and Use of Spiritual Gifts）

门诺弟兄会确信，信徒在归信基督的那一刻便领受了圣灵；圣灵的赐予绝非得救之后另一次单独发生的“第二重经历”（second experience）。但我们坚信信徒需要持续不断、反复多次地“被圣灵充满”。在教会中承担领袖职责的人，必须传讲并教导与门诺弟兄会在这一领域的理解和实践相一致的真理。

我们确信属灵恩赐的真实性与重要性。鉴于当代人日程过度繁忙以及普遍面临“精疲力竭”（burnout）的困境，个别基督徒需要获得充分的机会来发现并运用自己的恩赐，既造就他人，也实现自我生命的价值。凡在恩赐辨识上建立了有效机制的教会都会发现，其信徒在事奉中展现出更高的热情和更丰盛的果效。领袖必须以身作则，委身于成全圣徒、各尽其职，从而将关于恩赐的真理落到实处。教会必须鼓励、装备并赋能信徒在事奉中运用属灵恩赐。在这里，“事奉/事工”（ministry）的定义绝不仅限于在董事会或委员会中任职。事奉绝非牧师的专利；全体基督徒皆为事奉者（信徒皆祭司、人人皆有事奉职分）。

当我们地方教会在辨识与推选领袖时，对属灵恩赐的正确理解至关重要。选拔领袖必须基于属灵恩赐与基督化品格。在世俗世界中的世俗成就，绝不能保证一个人具备在教会中承担领导职分的属灵恩赐。

多年以来，门诺弟兄会普遍不鼓励运用“神迹性/表号性恩赐”（sign gifts），这多半是对极端灵恩主义/五旬节运动的一种反弹反应。然而在今天，我们大多数堂会都鼓励运用圣经所启示的一切恩赐。所有恩赐的运用都必须是为了造就基督的身体——教会，并且必须完全遵照哥林多前书12章和14章所立定的原则。各教会应当明确：没有任何一种恩赐是每一个基督徒都必须具备的，也没有任何一个基督徒能够单凭自己拥有全部的恩赐。

Baptism in the Spirit and Use of Spiritual Gifts

Mennonite Brethren believe that the Holy Spirit is received at the time of conversion, and that the gift of the Spirit is not a second experience subsequent to salvation. We do believe in the continual and repeated “filling with the Holy Spirit.” Those who give leadership in our churches need to teach what is consistent with Mennonite Brethren understanding and practice in this area. We believe in spiritual gifts. Given the contemporary concern about overly busy schedules and potential “burnout,” individual Christians need to be given opportunity to discover and use their gifts both for the building up of others and their own fulfillment. Churches that follow some pattern of gift discernment find that their people serve with more enthusiasm and a greater sense of fruitfulness. The understanding of the gifts must be fleshed out by leaders who are committed to preparing God’s people for works of service. The church must encourage, equip, and empower people to use their spiritual gifts in ministry. Ministry is defined here as much more than merely serving on boards or committees. Ministry is not performed only by pastors. All Christians are ministers.

Our understanding of giftedness is important when the local church discerns leaders. Leaders should be chosen on the basis of gifts and character. Success in the world does not guarantee that a person is spiritually gifted for leadership in the church.

For many years, Mennonite Brethren generally discouraged the use of sign gifts, perhaps as a reaction to extreme pentecostalism. Today, however, use of all gifts is encouraged in most of our congregations. The practice of all gifts needs to be for the sake of the building up of the body of Christ, the church, and according to the principles given in 1 Corinthians 12 and 14. It is understood that no gift is essential for every Christian to have, and also that no Christian has all of the gifts by him or herself.

地方教会与联合总会的关系（The Relationship of Local Church and Conference）

各地方堂会自主治理自身事务，但同时也隶属于所属的宗派大家庭。教会的工作应当在相互依存、彼此相爱、彼此顺服的精神中展开。在实践中，当个别信徒或个别堂会带着过度独立的倾向行事时，这种关系往往会变得紧张而艰难。当地方堂会内部出现问题时——例如在教义分歧或教牧领导风格上产生冲突——情况尤其令人尴尬。

牧师与地方教会必须明白：正因为我们共同立约结为联合总会（conference），我们在彼此之间就负有守望问责的责任。当受托承担总会/区会领导职责的同工对某一地方堂会内部的问题产生关切、并感到有责任进行介入协调时，地方堂会的成员应当敞开心扉接纳这种介入协助。与此同时，地方堂会的成员与领袖也应当坦然邀请总会/区会领袖协助化解棘手难题。彼此守望（互为问责），是基督徒生命不可或缺的一部分。

地方堂会有时会呼吁其会友在奉献上优先支持本堂，因为所有成员都曾对该堂会作出过委身承诺。那么，这同一座地方堂会，又岂能问心无愧地扣留其作为总会成员本已承诺支持的联合宣教事工之奉献款项？圣约共同体的范围绝不仅止于单一的地方堂会。

The Relationship of Local Church and Conference

Each congregation regulates its own affairs, but also affiliates with the denomination. The work of the church is to be conducted in a spirit of interdependence, love, and submission to one another. In practice, this becomes difficult when individuals or congregations operate with an overly independent spirit. It becomes especially awkward when a local church is having internal problems, as over doctrinal issues or the style of pastoral leadership. Pastors and local churches need to understand that because we have covenanted to be a conference together, we are also accountable to each other. When those charged with conference leadership sense a concern in the affairs of a local church and feel a need to intervene, the members of the local church should open themselves to such intervention. At the same time, members and leaders of the local church should feel freedom to invite conference leaders to assist in the resolution of difficult problems. Mutual accountability is part of the Christian life.

A congregation will sometimes appeal to its members to give the local church priority in their giving because all members have made a commitment to that church. Can that same local church, with integrity, hold back giving to ministries we have committed ourselves to as a conference? The covenant community extends beyond the local church.

地方教会内部的彼此守望（Accountability in the Local Church）

在地方教会中还存在着另一种维度的守望问责，即教会成员彼此之间相互承担的责任与守望。在一个日益多元化的社会中，加入地方教会的人必须明白：他们已经同意在个人品行、灵命光景、家庭生活、道德操守、商业往来、伦理抉择等多重领域中，接受彼此的守望与劝戒。藉着教会领袖的讲台宣讲与亲身垂范，这种基于“圣约共同体”委身的彼此守望，必须得到忠实的教导与切实的施行。牧师需要向总会/区会的领袖负责守望，而教会成员反过来也应当向地方教会的领袖顺服守望。

曾经有一位身陷罪错的人对她的牧师说：“我很庆幸自己不是正式会友，所以教会管不着我，不能对我施行纪律。”数年之后，当她的心意发生回转，她主动找到牧师请求加入教会会籍。她寻求加入教会的理由正是为了“有人能守望我、向我问责”，因为她深切认识到，彼此守望对于她自身的保护与灵命成长是何等必要。我们教会的所有成员都应当具备这样的共识与领悟。

教会纪律是彼此守望问责的有机组成部分。当得知某位基督徒卷入犯罪行为时，主内肢体或教会领袖应当本着圣经原则，怀着爱心前去与该肢体沟通。要同时兼顾公义审断与怜悯慈爱并非易事，但这正是所有纪律惩戒行动的终极目标。在门诺弟兄会的历史中，教会纪律经历了剧烈的摆荡：从过去往往严苛冷酷的时代，跌入当今普遍松懈涣散、甚至荡然无存的境地。如今正是重新找回健康平衡的历史时刻。因着地方教会本质上是一个圣约团契，堂会会众完全有权柄、也有责任介入其成员的生活给予牧养劝勉。

Accountability in the Local Church

There is another kind of accountability in the local church, the accountability of church members to and for each other. In an increasingly diverse society, it is important for people who join a local church to understand that they have agreed to make themselves accountable to each other in a number of areas involving personal, spiritual, family, moral, business, ethical, and other matters. Through the preaching and example of church leaders, mutual accountability based on our commitment to be a covenant community needs to be taught and practiced. Pastors need to make themselves accountable to their leaders at the conference level, and in turn church members need to be accountable to their local church leaders. Someone involved in wrongdoing once told her pastor, “I am glad I am not a member so that the church can’t discipline me.” Some years later after a change of heart, she came to the pastor and asked to join the church. Her reason for seeking membership was to be held accountable, recognizing that accountability was necessary for her protection and growth. All members of our churches should have such an understanding.

Church discipline is part of accountability. Where a Christian is believed to be involved in sinful actions, fellow believers or church leaders need to lovingly approach that person in accordance with biblical principles. It is not easy to practice both judgment and mercy, but that is the goal of all disciplinary action. Church discipline for Mennonite Brethren has changed from a time when it was often harsh to a day when it is often lax or even nonexistent. It is time to recover a healthier balance. The congregation has freedom to intervene in the lives of its members because of the covenantal nature of the local church.

地方教会的组织架构（Organization of the Local Church）

门诺弟兄会对于地方信徒群体的组织模式并未规定硬性一律的结构。各堂会可自行抉择其治理体制与领袖架构。虽然大多数门诺弟兄会教会由某种形式的长老会（elder board）或堂会理事会/执事会（church council）进行治理，但在所有这些体制中，全堂会众在重大决策上都被赋予发言权与实质参与权。

信徒互为弟兄姐妹的真理意味着：地方教会在涉及发展方向与组织架构的重大事项上，应当投入充足的时间在全体会友中充分沟通讨论，而非单凭少数几位领袖的独断专行来强推变革。坦诚开诚的沟通通常能够极大提升全体成员对新理念与新异象的认同感与主人翁意识（ownership）。虽然在确立新方向上寻求会众的共识需要付出额外的时间，但从长远来看，这往往会带来全教会更广泛的参与和更坚定的委身。圣经将教会描绘为“身体”和“家庭”。这两个称谓都强调了肢体之间清晰通畅的沟通。在这些圣经原则的指引下，各地方教会完全可以确立最能契合自身牧养需要的组织与领袖体制。

Organization of the Local Church

Mennonite Brethren do not have a prescribed pattern for organization of a local body of believers. Congregations choose their own form of government and their own structure of leadership. While most MB churches are governed by some form of elder board or church council, in all these the larger congregation is given voice and involvement in making major decisions. The brother and sisterhood of believers implies that local churches take the time to discuss matters of direction and organization among the members of the congregation, rather than implementing changes solely by decision of a few. Open communication usually enhances the ownership of new ideas and plans. While it takes extra time to secure agreement of the congregation on a new direction, it often leads to greater involvement and commitment in the long run. The Scriptures describe the church as a body and a family. Both terms speak of communication and clarity between the members involved. Within such guidelines, each local church can determine the patterns of organization and leadership which best serve its needs.

🏠 返回 Evan's Thinking 首页

ARTICLE 07 门诺弟兄会信仰宣言 · 中英双语对照典藏本

第 07 条：教会的使命 (Mission of the Church)

Article 7: Mission of the Church

信条条文 一、信仰宣言条文
Confession of Faith The Confession of Faith
🇨🇳 中文信条条文
🇬🇧 Original Confession Text

大使命与大诫命 (The Great Commission and the Great Commandment)

我们坚信，上帝在耶稣基督里的救恩福音是赐给全人类的。基督吩咐教会往普天下去使万民作祂的门徒：呼召人悔改，给他们施洗，并教导他们遵行耶稣的一切吩咐。耶稣教导门徒，当藉着传扬福音好消息以及践行爱与怜悯的行动，来爱上帝与爱邻舍。

The Great Commission and the Great Commandment

We believe the good news of God’s salvation in Jesus Christ is for all people. Christ commands the church to make disciples of all nations by calling people to repent, and by baptizing and teaching them to obey Jesus. Jesus teaches that disciples are to love God and neighbour by telling the good news and by doing acts of love and compassion.

见证 (The Witness)

圣灵赋予每位基督徒能力，为上帝的救恩作见证。教会作为一个身体，在世上见证上帝的统治与掌权。藉着其作为蒙救赎与分别出来的圣约团契的生命，教会向世界显明上帝施行拯救的美意。

The Witness

The Holy Spirit empowers every Christian to witness to God’s salvation. The church as a body witnesses to God’s reign in the world. By its life as a redeemed and separated community, the church reveals God’s saving purposes to the world.

📖 经文引证 (和合本 CUV)

马太福音 5:13-16；22:34-40；28:18-20；马可福音 1:15；12:28-34；路加福音 10:25-37；24:45-49；约翰福音 20:21-23；使徒行传 1:8；罗马书 1:16-18；哥林多后书 5:18-20；以弗所书 3:10-11。

📖 Scripture References

Matthew 5:13-16; 22:34-40; 28:18-20; Mark 1:15; 12:28-34; Luke 10:25-37; 24:45-49; John 20:21-23; Acts 1:8; Romans 1:16-18; 2 Corinthians 5:18-20; Ephesians 3:10-11.

神学注释 二、神学研读注释
Theological Commentary Commentary
🇨🇳 中文神学注释
🇬🇧 English Commentary

新旧约圣经中上帝的救赎计划，在教会的使命中得以延续并达到成全。旧约圣经为基督的宣教使命预备了舞台；新约福音书则带领我们经历基督的生平、教导、受死与复活，这些历史事件最终导向使万民作门徒的使命托付。使徒行传和书信记录了新约教会如何践行大使命的历程。正如新约教会所树立的典范，基督教会自诞生伊始，其首要任务就是将福音传遍地极。

The redemptive design of both the Old and New Testament finds its continuance and fulfillment in the mission of the church. The Old Testament prepares the stage for Christ’s mission. The New Testament Gospels take us through the events of Christ’s life and teaching, death and resurrection, which lead up to the mission assignment of making disciples of all nations. The book of Acts and the Epistles tell the story of how the church implements the Great Commission. The primary task of the Christian church from its inception has been the propagation of the gospel to the ends of the earth, as exemplified by the New Testament church.

上帝对教会的使命蓝图 (God’s Mission Design for the Church)

圣经中有多处生动的描绘，帮助我们聚焦于上帝为教会所设立的使命蓝图。诸如“盐”、“光”、“香气”或“荐信”（公开的信件）等比喻，强烈表明信徒当在其所处的影响范围内发挥积极的感化力；而诸如“使者”、“见证人”、“使人和睦者”（和平使者）、“得人的渔夫”以及“与上帝同工者”等行动性比喻，则进一步阐明了上帝对信徒的期望与设计。

圣经中的历史记叙，如耶路撒冷教会人数天天增加（徒 2:41, 47）、安提阿教会见证大批人归向主（徒 11:21），以及帖撒罗尼迦教会向上帝坚定的信心传遍各处（帖前 1:8），都生动呈现了教会使命的焦点所在。保罗在以弗所书中教导，要运用基督身体各样恩赐来成全圣徒，预备他们积极投身宣教事奉（弗 4:11-12），这也强有力地支持了教会以宣教为导向的特质。

真正有效的宣教必须涵盖言语与行动。圣经在展现上帝之国时的宏图，将大使命（太 28:18-20）与大诫命（太 22:37-40）紧密相连。无论在旧约还是新约时代，上帝的子民在一个充满贫困、不义与绝望的社会中，向来以流露仁爱与恩慈著称。上帝吩咐祂的子民生活在圣约关系之中——上帝是我们的上帝，我们是祂的子民，在世人面前彰显恩典、公义、信心与行为。

God’s Mission Design for the Church

There are various biblical descriptions that help focus God’s mission design for the church. Metaphors such as being salt, light, a fragrance, or an open letter strongly suggest having an impact within one’s sphere of influence. Metaphors of action such as being ambassadors, witnesses, reconcilers, fishers of men, and co-labourers with God help clarify God’s design for believers. Descriptions such as the Jerusalem church growing in numbers daily (Acts 2:41), the Antioch church seeing great numbers of people turning to the Lord (Acts 11:21), and the Thessalonian church having their faith in God known everywhere (1 Thessalonians 1:8) help to focus on the mission of the church. Paul’s teaching to the Ephesians to prepare God’s people for active mission with all the gifts of Christ’s body (Ephesians 4:11-12) gives support to the missional design of the church.

Effective mission involves both word and deed. The scriptural design for representing God’s kingdom connects the Great Commission (Matthew 28:18-20) with the Great Commandment (Matthew 22:37-40). In both the Old and New Testament, God’s people are known for their expressions of love and kindness within a society of poverty, injustice, and hopelessness. God’s people are mandated to live within a covenant relationship where God is our God and we are His people demonstrating grace, justice, faith, and works.

使命的预备 (The Mission in Preparation)

被称为“大使命”的宣教嘱托是在基督从死里复活后颁布的。然而，这道命令的实质内容对基督的门徒而言并不陌生。早在基督呼召门徒跟从祂时，祂就预先指出他们将要成为“得人的渔夫”（可 1:17；2:17）。随后，祂赐给他们制伏污鬼的权柄与能力，差遣他们去传讲上帝的国并医治病人（路 9:1-2, 5-6）。大使命的核心萌芽，在基督为世上信徒献上的大祭司祷告中亦清晰可见（约 17章）。

当基督颁布使万民作门徒的使命时，祂藉着言教与身传赋予福音真实的内涵，并宣告了跟从祂与服事祂的代价。在如何与人交往互动上，基督为门徒树立了完全的榜样——无论面对穷人、富人、无助者、患病者、达官显贵、好奇的群氓，还是默默无闻的普通人。基督在生活方式的见证上是他们完美的典范。

The Mission in Preparation

The mission mandate known as the Great Commission was given after Christ rose from the dead. The content of the mandate was not new to the followers of Christ. When Christ called his disciples to follow him, he indicated that they would become fishers of men (Mark 2:17). Later he gave them power and authority over demons and sent them to proclaim the kingdom of God and to heal (Luke 9:5-6). The seed thoughts of the Great Commission were also clearly evident in Christ’s prayer for the believers in the world ( John 17).

When Christ gave the mandate to make disciples of all nations, he gave meaning to the gospel both in words and by example and declared the cost of following and serving him. Christ was a model to his disciples in how he interacted with people, including the poor, the rich, the helpless, the sick, the prominent, the curious masses, and the hidden individuals. Christ was their model in lifestyle witness.

使命的颁布 (The Mission Declared)

大使命的托付（太 28:18-20）是一篇得胜的宣告，出自刚刚藉着牺牲之死与得胜复活战胜罪恶和死亡世界的基督之口。此时此刻，全世界得救已成为可能，因此祂发出命令：“所以，你们要去，使万民作我的门徒。”自第一世纪以来，这份大使命宣告便被誉为伟大的宣教宪章与教会的核心使命宣言。大使命在以下几个重要方面聚焦了教会的生命与工作：

  1. 宣告基督拥有天上地下所有的权柄：这使祂独具资格向教会颁布宣教托付。没有任何其他权柄能够抗衡并挑战君王基督。
  2. 明确以“造就门徒”作为宣教成果的衡量标准：使命的核心任务是培育出能够继续造就他人的门徒。这蕴含着一种深度的委身，要求信徒真诚、认真地跟从基督。
  3. 命令宣教托付必须具有普世性（面向全球）：不论任何种族，所有人都要被招聚进入上帝的国度。这是一项没有任何界限或例外的、具有全然包容性的宣教异象。
  4. 将宣教责任与主动权赋予所有信徒：每一位相信的人都被期待成为见证人和门徒培育者。
  5. 期望信徒藉着洗礼表明身份：大使命将洗礼确立为基督徒作门徒的标志。它象征着真诚悔改，以及对上帝之国度身体的全身心委身。
  6. 将新信徒描绘为信仰的学习者与践行者：大使命勾勒出了基督徒门徒生活的真实风貌。
  7. 应许基督在推展普世宣教使命的过程中与教会同工并常相伴随，直到世界的末了：基督始终作为救主与灵感力量的源泉，在教会的宣教使命中与教会保持着血脉相连的生命连接。

大使命以基督的权柄为开端，以基督恒常的同在为告终。这项不容妥协的使命托付，必须由所有属于基督的人在普世范围内付诸实施。该任务的目标极其明确：造就门徒，即塑造竭力跟从基督的人。推动这项宣教使命的首要动机，与其说是未信者的悲惨处境或信徒自身的永恒福乐，不如说是教会领受的圣洁呼召——尽心爱邻舍，并使万民作基督的门徒。

The Mission Declared

The Great Commission mandate (Matthew 28:18-20) is a victory statement that flows from the lips of Christ who had just overcome the world of sin and death through his sacrificial death and victorious resurrection. It is now possible for the entire world to be saved, hence the command, “Go and make disciples of all nations.” This Great Commission statement has been hailed as the great missionary charter and the church’s mission statement since the first century. The Great Commission brings focus to the church’s life and work in a number of ways: It declares Christ as having all authority over heaven and earth, qualifying him to give the mission assignment to the church. No other authority can challenge Christ the King.

It clarifies the mission results in terms of disciples made. The task is to make disciples who will disciple others. There is a commitment implied that involves serious followership.

It commands the mission assignment to be worldwide. All people are to be gathered into the kingdom regardless of race. It is an all-inclusive mission vision with no limits or exceptions.

It assigns mission responsibility and initiative to all believers. Everyone who believes is expected to be a witness and a discipler.

It expects the believers to be identified by baptism. The Great Commission makes baptism a sign of Christian discipleship. It signals sincerity and commitment to the kingdom body.

It describes the new followers as learners and practitioners of the faith. A life of Christian discipleship is profiled in the Great Commission mandate.

It promises Christ’s partnership and presence in the implementation of the worldwide mission assignment, until the very end. Christ remains vitally connected as the Saviour and the inspiration in the midst of the church’s mission assignment.

The Great Commission starts with Christ’s authority and concludes with Christ’s continuous presence. This non-negotiable assignment is to be carried out worldwide by all those who belong to Christ. The assignment is clearly focused in its purpose of making disciples or diligent followers of Christ. The primary motive for this mission assignment is not so much the plight of the unconverted or the eternal blessing of the believer. Its primary motive is the church’s calling to love neighbours and to make disciples of all nations.

使命的践行 (The Mission Implemented)

圣经期望教会在社会中参与救赎性的行动，以此作为上帝之国临在世上的表记。基督的身体藉着言行相符的整全见证（an integrated witness of deeds and words），确证其信仰的真实性。

1. 委身的见证 (The Witness of Commitment)

在群体中活出圣洁生命是一股强大的见证力量。当社会目睹教会在世上作为一个经历更新转变的共同体生活时，教会就是在忠心活出耶稣对信徒“作盐作光”的描绘（太 5:14-16）。藉着敬虔、敬拜与信心的生命对圣经朴素真诚的顺服，赋予了基督徒人生无可置疑的公信力。教会坚贞不渝的忠心，能使那些伺机挑剔基督徒言行不一的批评者哑口无言，并对其心灵产生深远震撼。这种忠信的见证是践行大使命的第一步。

2. 爱心的见证 (The Witness of Love)

活出信仰极其具体实际的一面，就是爱邻舍（可 12:31）。遵行基督命令而活出的基督徒爱心见证，乃是上帝爱世人最有力的彰显。我们的爱必须在邻里社区、工作职场与家庭生活中找到切实的表达。基督的身体藉着爱的善行与为他人益处甘心倾倒自己的慷慨舍己，来彰显其信仰。

当世人观察到信仰群体展现关怀与怜悯——保护弱势群体、为无权无势者辩护、救助贫困与受创伤者时，教会就真实流露出了基督对弱势群体的怜悯心肠。基督为教会树立了典范，呼召教会效法祂的榜样去关怀困苦者、喂养饥饿者、释放被掳者并医治患病者（路 4:18-19）。

3. 团契共同体的见证 (The Witness of Community)

教会的群体生活本身对旁观的世界就是强有力的见证。当教会在合一与相爱中运转时，它就映照出上帝的形象。基督身体的日常生活在众人面前散发出基督馨香之气（林后 2:15）。教会的合一具有强大的吸引力（约 17:23）。在一个对空谈厌倦的社会中，真情流露的喜乐、团契相交与敬拜拥有巨大的感染力。彼此关怀与同心代祷则是群体见证的另外两个重要维度。

4. 和平的见证 (The Witness of Peace)

信仰群体还肩负着在一个充满疏离与暴力的社会中展现救赎之爱的使命。上帝之国的特质藉着和平与公义的行动显明出来。基督徒需要站在弱势者与灰心绝望者的一边。以和平方式替代暴力冲突，以及挺身捍卫无能为力者的行动，其本身就是爱与良善的有力宣告。跟从和平之君基督的人，也必在其所在的世界中成为和平的化身与使者。

5. 言语的见证 (The Witness of Words)

上帝的灵赋予基督的身体放胆讲论基督福音的能力（徒 1:8；4:31）。个人生命改变的经历与见证具有极强的震撼力。聚集的教会也需要清晰宣告基督降世为人类成就的救恩。每位基督徒都有一个故事可讲——见证基督如何赦免罪恶，并赐予生命全新的目标与意义。

教会整体与个别信徒相结合的见证，正是这世界迫切需要听见的（林后 3:2）。言语的见证唯有与其他层面的见证交织融合时，方能爆发出沛然莫之能御的力量。我们的见证若要结出硕果，就必须融入全人生活的方方面面。这并非意味着善行能拯救我们（弗 2:5, 8-9），而是当我们站在上帝面前接受赏赐时，我们的行为将印证我们信心的真实无伪（太 25:31-46）。

The Mission Implemented

The Scriptures expect the church to be involved in redemptive action in society as a sign of God’s kingdom in the world. The body of Christ authenticates its faith through an integrated witness of deeds and words. The Witness of Commitment: Holy life in community is a powerful witness. When society sees the church living as a transformed community in the world, the church is being faithful to Jesus’ description of believers as salt and light (Matthew 5:14-16). Simple obedience to the Scriptures through lives of devotion, worship, and faith gives credibility to the Christian life. The steadfast faithfulness of the church disarms and impacts would-be critics who are looking for inconsistencies among Christians. This witness of faithfulness is the first step in fulfilling the Great Commission.

The Witness of Love: A very practical part of living out our faith is that of loving our neighbour (Mark 12:31). This witness of Christian love as commanded by Christ is a powerful representation of God’s love for the world. Our love needs to find practical expressions in our neighbourhoods, workplaces, and homes. The body of Christ demonstrates its faith through deeds of love and the generous giving of self to the interests of others.

When the world observes the faith community showing concern and compassion by protecting the vulnerable, defending the powerless, and helping the poor and hurting, they have truly expressed the heartbeat that Christ had for the underprivileged. Christ provides a paradigm for the church to follow his example of caring for the needy, feeding the hungry, liberating the captives, and healing the sick (Luke 4:18-19).

The Witness of Community: The corporate life of the church is in itself a powerful witness to the watching world. As the church functions in unity and love, it reflects the image of God. The daily life of the body of Christ is a fragrance to others (2 Corinthians 2:15). The unity of the church attracts ( John 17:23). In a word-weary society, authentic expressions of joy, fellowship, and worship have great impact. Caring and praying are two further dimensions of community witness. The Witness of Peace: The community of faith is also about showing redemptive love within a society of alienation and violence. Kingdom characteristics express themselves through acts of peace and justice. Christians need to stand on the side of those who are disadvantaged and discouraged. Peaceful alternatives to violence and actions to defend the powerless become statements of love and goodness in themselves. Followers of Christ the Prince of Peace must also be expressions of peace in their world.

The Witness of Words: The Spirit of God empowers the body of Christ to speak boldly regarding the good news of Christ (Acts 1:5). Personal stories and testimonies of changed lives are very powerful. The church gathered also needs to present a clear statement of what Christ came to do for humankind. Each Christian has a story to tell of how Christ forgives sin and gives new purpose and meaning for life. The combined witness of the church body and of individual Christians is what the world needs to hear (2 Corinthians 3:2). The witness of words flows with power when joined with the other aspects of witness. For our witness to be fruitful it needs to be integrated into all of life. Not that our works save us (Ephesians 2:5), but our deeds will identify the genuineness of our faith when we appear before God to receive our rewards (Matthew 25:31-46).

使命的必备要素 (The Mission Requirements)

基督的教会若要在落实大使命上大有成效，就必须与圣经所启示的有效外展要求保持一致。圣经的记载为多结果子的见证与福音宣教提供了若干不可或缺的核心要素：

  • 圣灵的赋能 (Empowerment of the Holy Spirit, 徒 1:8)：正是上帝的灵赋予信徒作见证的能力。圣灵引导、启迪、提醒、叫人知罪自责、代求并赐予新生命。圣灵也敞开见证的门，并藉着圣灵的果子与属灵恩赐使我们满有吸引力且事奉有力。
  • 对福音的确信 (Confidence in the Gospel, 罗 1:16; 10:17)：有力的见证源于深信福音本是上帝的大能，要救一切相信的。耶稣是唯一能够赦罪的救主，这一福音乃是绝佳的好消息。信道是从听道来的。因此，我们不以福音为耻。
  • 信徒之间的合一与相爱 (Unity and Love Among Believers, 约 17:23; 弗 4章)：基督明确指出，信徒之间的相爱与合一是吸引人归向基督的强大驱动力。一间纷争撕裂或分门结党的教会，无法给寻求真理的世界提供任何有价值的盼望。相爱与合一蕴含着基督在祷告中所企盼的福音吸引力。
  • 对基督忠心的顺服 (Faithful Obedience to Christ, 约 15章; 加 5章)：基督将结果子与忠心顺服紧密相连。基督徒若常在葡萄树上并与主紧密相连，就必经历喜乐并经历祷告蒙应允。藉着顺从圣灵而行所流露出的基督荣美样式，能使我们在言语和行为的生命中大有能力。缺乏诚信正直会彻底毁坏见证，而祷告与信心则能极大提升见证的力量。
  • 充满爱的关系 (Love Relationships, 路 10:27; 可 12:31)：爱邻舍是引领人归向基督最主要的桥梁。藉着仁慈善行表达的爱，为建立富有成效的关怀关系创造了良善的契机。当教会中的人对身旁之人显出爱心、关怀与尊重时，他们就映照出了基督的心肠。
  • 教会的首要任务 (Priority for the Church, 约 20:21-23)：正如耶稣降世是为要寻找、拯救失丧的人，教会同样受差遣投身于这项救赎性的使命。这一优先要务必须在个人与教会群体两个层面同时体现出来。信徒个人无权自私度日，教会同样无权将信仰秘藏于自身墙内。在群体层面，教会有责任主动创造机会，让世人得以听见并回应基督的福音信息。

The Mission Requirements

For the church of Christ to be effective in the implementation of the Great Commission, it needs to align itself with the biblical requirements for effective outreach. The records of Scripture provide a number of indispensable realities for fruitful witness and evangelism.

Empowerment of the Holy Spirit (Acts 1:8): It is the Spirit of God that empowers the believer for witness. The Spirit guides, enlightens, reminds, convicts, intercedes, and provides new life. The Spirit also opens witnessing doors and makes us attractive and effective through the fruit of the Spirit and spiritual gifts.

Confidence in the Gospel (Romans 1:16; 10:17): Effective witness is the result of being confident that the gospel is the power of God for salvation. The gospel of Jesus as the only Saviour who can forgive sins is good news. Faith comes from hearing the word. Therefore, we are not ashamed of the gospel.

Unity and Love Among Believers ( John 17:23; Ephesians 4): Christ made it clear that love and unity among believers is a powerful incentive for people to come to Christ. A disruptive or disunited church has little to offer a seeking world. Love and unity have evangelistic drawing power for which Christ prayed.

Faithful Obedience to Christ ( John 15; Galatians 5): Christ links fruitfulness to faithfulness. If Christians remain in the vine and keep connected to the Lord, they will experience joy and answers to their prayers. Christlikeness through keeping in step with the Spirit brings power to our life of words and deeds. Witness is destroyed through lack of integrity but enhanced through prayer and faith.

Love Relationships (Luke 10:27; Mark 12:31): Loving one’s neighbour is the main bridge to winning people to Christ. Expressions of love through deeds of kindness create an environment for effective relationships. When those in the church show love, care, and respect for others around them, they reflect the spirit of Christ.

Priority for the Church ( John 20:21-23): As Jesus came to seek and to save the lost, so the church has been sent on a redemptive mission. This priority must be expressed both individually and corporately. The church has no more right to keep the faith to itself than individuals have the right to live selfishly. At the corporate level, the church takes responsibility to provide opportunities for the world to hear and respond to the message of Christ.

使命的紧迫性 (The Mission Urgencies)

基督在托付教会使命时带着强烈的紧迫感。以下若干事实激励着教会在履行使命上保持忠贞不渝：

  1. 人是可以蒙救赎的：上帝爱世人，愿意所有人都得享永生，并在祂的旨意与同在赋能之下生活。人能够脱离罪的奴役重获自由，得享充满喜乐、平安与意义的人生（约 3:16；林后 5:17）。
  2. 在耶稣基督之外别无拯救：耶稣是通往天父的唯一道路。基督徒受召向世人宣扬基督就是道路、真理、生命（约 14:6；徒 16:31）。
  3. 若离开基督里的救恩，人就失丧在罪中并被定罪：福音的大喜信息在于：耶稣已经为我们的罪代死，并将赦罪之恩作为白白的礼物赐给一切相信的人（罗 3:23；5:1）。
  4. 使万民作门徒是基督的命令：上帝的旨意是要世人听信真道。属信徒世界的核心任务，是使万民认识基督。耶稣的使命已成为每一间教会的优先要务（太 28:18-20）。
  5. 主的日子即将临到，唯有接受基督的人才能确获永生：在预备朝见我们的上帝这件事上，时间的紧迫性刻不容缓（林前 15:50-58）。

The Mission Urgencies

Christ presented the mission of the church with a sense of urgency. There are a number of realities that motivate the church to faithfulness in its mission: People are redeemable. God loves the world and wants all people to have eternal life and to live a life empowered by His purpose and presence. People can be set free and given a life of joy, peace, and purpose ( John 3:16; 2 Corinthians 5:17).

There is no salvation outside of Jesus Christ. Jesus is the only way to the Father. Christians are called to proclaim Christ as the way, the truth, and the life ( John 14:6; Acts 16:31).

People are lost and condemned in their sins without salvation in Christ. The good news is that Jesus has died for our sins and offers forgiveness as a free gift to all who believe (Romans 3:23; 5:1).

To make disciples is a command of Christ. The will of God is for the world to hear and believe. The primary task for the believing world is to make Christ known among all the nations. The mission of Jesus has become the mission priority of every church (Matthew 28:18-20).

The day of the Lord is coming and only those who have accepted Christ are assured of life eternal. There is an urgency of time in preparing to meet our God (1 Corinthians 15:50-58).

结语 (Conclusion)

尽管我们为基督所作的见证以多元的形式展现，但无论是教会群体还是信徒个人，都有着明确的救赎目的。上帝之国的福音在大诫命与大使命中得到了彻底的成全。

Conclusion

Even though our witness for Christ is expressed in various ways, both corporately and individually we have a clear redemptive purpose. The gospel of God’s kingdom finds complete fulfillment in the Great Commandment and the Great Commission.

教牧应用 三、教牧实践应用
Pastoral Application Pastoral Application
🇨🇳 中文教牧应用
🇬🇧 English Pastoral Application

以下三个核心视角有助于我们深入理解教会的使命：

第一，回转归主绝非终点本身。 使徒约翰劝勉我们：“人若说他住在主里面，就该自己照主所行的去行”（约壹 2:6）。造就门徒被明确定义为“凡我所吩咐你们的，都教训他们遵守”（太 28:20）。传福音的布道任务绝不可与培育门徒（nurture）的任务割裂开来。健康的教会赋予信徒能力，使他们在对外拓展与对内造就中皆能充分发挥恩赐。基督徒在世上既要做盐，也要做光（太 5:13-16）。

第二，与世俗流行观念相反，耶稣的呼召绝非孤立的个人主义救赎。 相反，它包含着加入“委身者团契”（the company of the committed）、成为基督身体的一个肢体、有份于“圣徒相通”（fellowship of the saints）的呼召。在我们这个四分五裂、充斥着个人主义的世界中，这个群体性维度（corporate dimension）绝不容被忽视。它本身就是上帝在世上临在与统治的有力明证。

第三，这一群体维度对福音宣教同样至关重要。 正是教会为福音传播提供了处境（context）。基督身体的肢体彼此如何相待、如何活出信仰，构成了极具感染力的见证。它构建了一个“合理性结构”（plausibility structure），使不信者能够从中理解并信服福音的真实。它向世界呈现了一个“另类社会”（alternative society），在其中福音被生动地践行出来。

The following perspectives may be helpful in our understanding of the mission of the church. First, conversion is not an end in itself. We are admonished by the apostle John, “Whoever claims to live in him must walk as Jesus did” (1 John 2:6). Disciple making is described as “teaching them to obey everything I have commanded” (Matthew 28:20). The evangelistic task must never be separated from the nurturing task. Healthy churches empower people to use their gifts both in outreach and in nurture. Christians are to be both salt and light in the world (Matthew 5:13-16).

Second, contrary to popular perceptions, the call of Jesus is not merely an individualistic call to salvation. Rather, it includes a call to join “the company of the committed,” to become a member of the body, to share in the “fellowship of the saints.” This corporate dimension dare not be omitted in our shattered individualistic world. It is the testimony of God’s presence and rule in the world.

Third, this corporate dimension is also vital to evangelism. It is the church that offers the context for evangelism. How the members of the body relate to one another, how they live out their faith, provides a powerful witness. It creates a plausibility structure out of which unbelievers can make sense of the gospel. It offers the world an alternative society, one in which the gospel is actively worked out.

专职化的试探 (A Temptation to Specialize)

在一个个人主义与专业分工盛行的时代，人们对“专家”的崇拜往往诱使我们将大使命的践行推卸给教会专门指定的专职人员。在我们的传统中，这包括被称为布道家、宣教士与牧师的人士。

尽管我们必须承认这些被指定之仆人的特殊恩赐，但我们必须坚信并坚持：大使命涵盖了所有宣称跟从耶稣的人。事实上，我们作为一个宗派以及作为更广泛的再洗礼派（Anabaptist）传统成员的历史，其鲜明标志就是广大普通信徒普遍具备并分享着宣教热忱。

我们迫切需要重新寻回属灵先辈的热火与心志，他们深刻理解个人圣洁的要求不仅在于个人修身，更包括亲口传讲福音之道，并在日常生活中活出福音的深远含义。当代教会对受训专家的依赖，根源很可能在于我们对“方法与技巧”的过分迷恋。然而，一个“见证人”的本质，仅仅是述说他/她所亲身知道与经历的事。真正有力的见证不在于技巧的精巧，而在于忠贞的顺服、言行一致的生命，以及圣灵使人重生的再造大能。

A Temptation to Specialize

In an age of individualism and specialization, deference to the “expert” tempts us to relegate the execution of the Great Commission to those designated by the church for that purpose. In our tradition, this includes those called evangelists, missionaries, and pastors.

While we must recognize the special giftedness of designated servants, we must insist that the commission includes all those who claim to follow Jesus. Indeed, our history as a denomination and as members of the wider Anabaptist tradition is one marked by missionary zeal shared by a broad spectrum of people.

It is vital that we regain the zeal of our spiritual ancestors who understood the requirements of personal holiness to include speaking the words of the gospel and living out its implications in everyday life. It may be that the current reliance on the trained expert has its origin in our preoccupation with technique. A witness, however, is simply one who tells what he or she knows. Effective witness is found not in technique but in faithfulness, consistent living, and the regenerating power of the Holy Spirit.

兼具排他性与包容性的信息 (An Exclusive and Inclusive Message)

鉴于普救论（universalism）日益增长的吸引力，我们必须坚守救恩唯一的排他性道路：“除他以外，别无拯救；因为在天下人间，没有赐下别的名，我们可以靠着得救”（徒 4:12，和合本/NRSV）。我们必须抵制向当今世界的多元主义妥协退让的冲动。

与此同时，我们也要切记：在基督里得享新生命的呼召同样具有广阔的包容性——它是赐给“凡接待祂的”所有人。我们务要牢记使徒的劝勉：基督已经拆毁了中间隔断的墙（弗 2:14）。各个堂会应当更认真地审视自己所处的地理与社会处境。教会增长运动中的“同质原则”（homogenous unit principle）往往为我们在何处建立教会以及如何运作教会提供合理化借口。然而，我们如何在将福音传给“这弟兄中一个最小的”事上更加忠心呢？

宗派内的多元性绝不能仅仅停留在“隔离但平等”（separate but equal）的各民族/群体堂会层面。各地方堂会自身必须向世人彰显：社会、种族、经济与性别的藩篱确实已被彻底打破。即便世俗文化灌输“唯有隔离但平等的多元文化主义才行得通”，我们也必须见证：上帝使人和好的大爱能将各族各方的人招聚在一起，彼此相爱、互相关怀。这种“在多元中的合一”（unity in diversity），正是一个充满生机与活力的见证性信仰群体的鲜明印记。

教会的使命需要在地方教会的日常生活中体现出来。无论是在教会“聚集”（gathered）还是“分散”（scattered）的状态中，宣教使命都应在教会的氛围中自然流露。作为生命目的紧扣福音好消息的子民，我们信徒应当活出满有喜乐与盼望的人生。前来参加崇拜聚会的人完全有理由期望整个聚会洋溢着信心、盼望与仁爱。宣扬“好消息”的使命，应当在一切可能的方式中得以充分彰显。

An Exclusive and Inclusive Message

Given the increased attraction of universalism, we must insist on the exclusive way of salvation. “There is salvation in no one else, for there is no other name under heaven given among mortals by which we must be saved” (Acts 4:12 NRSV). We must resist the impulse to accommodate this focus to the pluralism of our world.

We do well to remember that the call to new life in Christ is also inclusive. It is to “all who receive him.” We must take care to remember the admonition of the apostle that the dividing wall has been broken down (Ephesians 2:14). It would be good for congregations to take a more serious look at where they are located. The homogenous principle of the church growth movement tends to justify where and how we do church. How might we be more faithful in bringing the good news to the “least of these”? Diversity needs to involve more than “separate but equal” congregations in the denomination. Individual congregations need to demonstrate that the social, racial, economic, and gender walls have indeed been broken down. Even when our culture teaches that only separate but equal multiculturalism works, we must demonstrate that God’s reconciling love brings every variety of people together to love and care for one another. This unity in diversity becomes the hallmark of a vibrant, witnessing community of faith. The mission of the church needs to find expression in the daily life of the local church. Mission should be revealed in the atmosphere of the church gathered and scattered. As a people whose purpose is built around good news, we believers should live joyful, hope-filled lives. People arriving at a worship service have reason to expect the event to radiate faith, hope, and love. The mission of “good news” should be reflected in every way possible.

双重的呼召：布道宣教与社会关怀 (A Two-Part Call)

当门诺弟兄会基督徒从“福音布道”与“社会关怀”两方面兼具的角度来理解福音时，他们在更广泛的基督徒群体中便享有一种独特而宝贵的地位。我们必须毫无保留地呼召人悔改，并在耶稣里经历生命的翻转与重塑。在宣讲这一信息时，我们必须做到清晰明确且在文化上易于被人理解；同时，我们要藉着关怀困苦之人并在更广大的社群中宣告上帝的和平与公义，切实活出福音的社会意涵。

上帝使人和好的慈爱福音是全人、整全的（holistic），涵盖人类经验的属灵、社会、人际关系与身体物质等各个层面。耶稣所成就的救赎大工，是要救赎包括属灵与物质在内的整个受造界。作为和好的使者，我们以言语和行动共同宣告基督救赎大能的整全性。我们必须递上一杯凉水，而且必须奉耶稣的名去递！

在历史上，门诺弟兄会一直是奉基督之名联合开展合作事工以满足人们物质需要的领头羊。尽管组织架构在历经变革，但我们与其他再洗礼派宗派通力合作的优良传统必须予以继承和发扬——诸如门诺会中央委员会（Mennonite Central Committee, MCC）、门诺灾难救助服务社（Mennonite Disaster Service, MDS）、门诺互助会（Mennonite Mutual Aid, MMA，现 Everence）、门诺经济发展联合会（Mennonite Economic Development Associates, MEDA）等机构的事工。爱邻舍与顺服基督的主权，既包括口头传扬上帝的爱，也包括藉着我们的实际行动彰显上帝的爱（太 25:31-46）。

A Two-Part Call

Mennonite Brethren Christians can enjoy a unique position in the wider Christian community when they understand the gospel in terms of both evangelism and social concern. We must without reservation call people to repentance and reformation of life in Jesus. We must be clear and culturally accessible in this proclamation and live out the social implications of the good news by caring for those in need and proclaiming God’s peace and justice in the wider community.

The news of God’s reconciling love is holistic, encompassing spiritual, social, relational, and physical aspects of human experience. The atonement that Jesus accomplished was to redeem the spiritual and physical creation. As ambassadors of reconciliation, we proclaim in word and deed the allencompassing nature of Christ’s redemptive power. We must give the cup of water and we must do it in Jesus’ name!

Historically, Mennonite Brethren have been leaders in cooperative ventures to meet physical needs in Christ’s name. Although structures are changing, the strong tradition of cooperating with other Anabaptist denominations in the work of Mennonite Central Committee, Mennonite Disaster Service, Mennonite Mutual Aid, Mennonite Economic Development Associates, and the like needs to be continued. Loving neighbour and obeying the lordship of Christ involves both communicating God’s love verbally and demonstrating it through our actions (Matthew 25:31-46).

培育健康的教会 (Growing a Healthy Church)

我们唯有在教会自身健康的前提下，才能圆满达成教会的使命。门诺弟兄会运用各种工具来促进堂会的健康。健康堂会生活的核心要素包括：

  1. 赋能型领导 (Empowering leadership)：一支清楚知道堂会前行方向的领袖团队，能够提供富有远见的领导。领袖们呼召信徒积极投身服事，激励他们，并装备他们承担事工。
  2. 恩赐导向的事奉 (Gift-oriented ministry)：这种恩赐的调动与整合，鼓励肢体运用自身所拥有的恩赐，去从事他们最得心应手、最有成效的事工。
  3. 充满热忱的灵性 (Passionate spirituality)：这是决定堂会健康与否的决定性因素。当基督徒满怀热忱、带着日益深厚的委身活出信仰时，教会的健康度就会不断提升。
  4. 功能健全的架构 (Functional structures)：保障教会健康运作与恩赐发挥的组织体系。
  5. 激发人心的敬拜 (Inspiring worship)：其特征在于信徒全情主动的参与，不论其崇拜形式是偏向礼仪性还是自由开放，偏向现代还是传统。
  6. 整全的小组 (Holistic small groups)：不断增长的堂会善用小组团契来提供一个具安全感的花园环境、建立亲密的人际关系，并提供分享属灵经历的平台。这些小组使主内弟兄姊妹在作门徒的道路上不断成长。
  7. 以需要为导向的布道 (Need-oriented evangelism)：以接触对象的实际需要为切入点的布道法，就是关系式布道（relational evangelism）。这绝不否定宣教布道以上帝为中心的本质（呼召人顺服上帝的至高主权），而是深刻认识到福音关照生命的每一个层面。
  8. 充满爱心的关系 (Loving relationships)：在健康成长的教会中，信徒之间的人际关系流露着高度的彼此相爱，并对教会内外的所有人倾注真挚关怀。

Growing a Healthy Church

We can accomplish the church’s mission only to the extent that the church itself is healthy. Mennonite Brethren use various tools to encourage congregational health. Essential elements for healthy congregational life include empowering leadership, gift-oriented ministry, passionate spirituality, functional structures, inspiring worship, holistic small groups, need-oriented evangelism, and loving relationships. A leadership team that knows where the congregation should be going provides visionary leadership. Leaders call out people for active ministry, motivate them, and equip them for ministry. This mobilization of gifts encourages members to use the gifts they have to do the work they enjoy most and can do most effectively.

The decisive factor in congregational health is passionate spirituality. When Christians live their faith enthusiastically and with growing commitment, the church grows in health. Inspiring worship is characterized by active participation by the people, whether the forms are more liturgical or free, more contemporary or traditional. Growing congregations use small groups to provide a secure environment, personal relationships, and the opportunity to share spiritual experiences. These small groups enable brothers and sisters in the faith to grow as disciples. Evangelism that uses as its starting point the needs of the people who are to be reached is relational evangelism. This does not negate the theocentricity of evangelism, calling people to submit to God’s sovereignty, but understands that the gospel addresses all aspects of life. In growing churches, the relationships of people to one another are characterized by a high degree of love and caring for those inside and outside the church.

与世界一样广阔的异象 (A Vision as Wide as the World)

从历史上看，我们向来将自己界定为一场“宣教运动”（missionary movement）。这一统摄全局的异象主题，激励了世界各地波澜壮阔的宣教壮举。这也解释了为何在工场上建立的“宣教区教会”（Mission Church）在规模上远远超过了后方的“差传母会”（Sending Church）。作为一个宗派，我们始终展现出甘愿为将福音传遍全球而奉献生命与财物的坚定决心。然而不可否认，我们当中的一些人可能在投身普世差传的同时，却忽略了向身边的邻舍以及本社区中与我们不同的人传扬好消息的本分。

在此，我们必须再次强调宣教使命的多维特性。对大使命（太 28:18-20）更贴切的原文字义理解很可能是：“你们无论去到哪里，都要使万民作门徒……”（As you are going, make disciples...，分词 poreuthentes 表明在日常生活的行进之中带出使命）。若然如此，这一宣教责任便落在了我们每一个人的肩上，无论我们往何处去。

然而，正因为跟从耶稣的呼召是面向所有人的，我们绝不能丧失差遣可靠见证人的紧迫感——不仅要差往耶路撒冷、犹太全地和撒玛利亚，更要差直到地极（徒 1:8）。地方教会有责任激发对困苦之人的怜悯心肠，培育关切失丧世界的宣教异象，为宣教同工与全地迫切代祷，并制定将工人差派到宣教工场的切实计划（太 9:35-10:1）。

A Vision as Wide as the World

Historically, we have defined ourselves as a missionary movement. This over-arching theme has inspired great missionary endeavours around the world. It may also explain why the “Mission Church” is considerably larger than the “Sending Church.” As a denomination, we have consistently shown ourselves ready to pledge our lives and our fortunes to spreading the gospel around the globe. Indeed, some of us may have done this to the exclusion of our responsibility to take the good news to our own neighbours and those who are different from us in our own community.

Here again, we must emphasize the multifaceted nature of our commission. “As you are going, make disciples...” is probably the correct reading of the Great Commission (Matthew 28:18-20). If so, the responsibility falls on all of us wherever we are going. Because the call to follow Jesus is to all, however, we must not lose the urgency of sending credible witnesses not only to Jerusalem, Judea, and Samaria, but also to the ends of the earth (Acts 1:8). It is the responsibility of the local church to foster compassion for those in need, a vision for the lost world, a prayer focus for mission workers and the world, and a plan for sending workers into mission (Matthew 9:35-10:1).

🏠 返回 Evan's Thinking 首页
