ARTICLE 10 门诺弟兄会信仰宣言 · 中英双语对照典藏本

第 10 条：门徒生涯 (Discipleship)

Article 10: Discipleship

信条条文 一、信仰宣言条文
Confession of Faith The Confession of Faith
🇨🇳 中文信条条文
🇬🇧 Original Confession Text

跟从耶稣（Following Jesus）

我们确信，耶稣呼召那些体验过重生喜乐的人作为门徒来跟从祂。基督借着呼召跟从祂的人背起十字架，邀请他们弃绝世俗不敬虔的价值观，并将自己奉献给上帝，过服事的生活。内住在每一位基督徒心里的圣灵，赐力量给信徒，使他们能够胜过罪恶本性的行为与态度。门徒满怀爱与感恩，以顺服上帝为乐。

Following Jesus

We believe that Jesus calls people who have experienced the joy of new birth to follow him as disciples. By calling his followers to take up the cross, Christ invites them to reject the godless values of the world and offer themselves to God in a life of service. The Holy Spirit, who lives in every Christian, empowers believers to overcome the acts and attitudes of the sinful nature. Filled with love and gratitude, disciples delight to obey God.

联结于独特的共同体（United in a Distinct Community）

基督徒享有与上帝及其他信徒相交的团契。在受洗时，信徒加入地方教会，立志建立基督的身体，并为基督徒盼望的福音作见证。在共同体中，肢体借着结出圣灵的果子、运用属灵恩赐，并在基督徒生活的诸般操练中操练彼此问责，从而在生命中渐渐成熟。基督徒在教会共同体的生活中认罪、悔改，并经历上帝的恩典。

United in a Distinct Community

Christians enjoy fellowship with God and other believers. At baptism, believers are joined to the local church, commit themselves to build up the body of Christ, and witness to the good news of the Christian hope. In community, members grow in maturity as they demonstrate the fruit of the Spirit, use their spiritual gifts, and practice mutual accountability in the disciplines of the Christian life. Christians confess sin, repent, and experience God’s grace in the life of the Christian community.

彰显真实的信心（Demonstrating True Faith）

耶稣教导说，门徒之路乃是舍己之路，并应许赐福给那些为义受苦的人。门徒当抵挡世俗的价值观与体系、罪恶的本性以及魔鬼。门徒慷慨施予，拒绝将财富奉为神明的物质主义（拜金主义）。门徒以怜悯与温和待人，拒绝以暴力手段回应不公义。门徒说诚实话以造就他人，拒绝虚谎、粗俗与轻浮妄语；他们力求避免诉诸法律诉讼来解决个人恩怨，尤其是在信徒之间。门徒持守性纯洁与婚姻忠贞，拒绝婚前与婚外的不道德性关系，以及一切同性性行为。作门徒，意味着在日常生活中对耶稣全然忠诚。

Demonstrating True Faith

Jesus teaches that discipleship is the way of self-denial and promises blessing for those who suffer for righteousness. Disciples are to resist worldly values and systems, the sinful nature, and the devil. Disciples give generously and reject materialism, which makes a god out of wealth. Disciples treat others with compassion and gentleness and reject violence as a response to injustice. Disciples speak honestly to build others up and reject dishonest, vulgar, and careless talk; they seek to avoid lawsuits to resolve personal grievances, especially with other believers. Disciples maintain sexual purity and marital faithfulness and reject immoral premarital and extramarital relationships and all homosexual practices. To be a disciple means to be true to Jesus in everyday life.

📖 经文引证 (和合本 CUV)

诗篇 1篇；119篇；阿摩司书 5:24；马太福音 5–7章；18:15–20；马可福音 8:34–38；约翰福音 8:31–32；13:34–35；15:14–15；使徒行传 2:41–47；罗马书 1:24–32；8:1–30；12章；哥林多前书 6:9–11；11:1；12:1–13；哥林多后书 8–9章；加拉太书 2:20；5:16–26；6:1–2；以弗所书 4:11–12, 15–16；5:1, 18；腓立比书 2:6–8；歌罗西书 3:1–17；帖撒罗尼迦前书 4:3–8；5:17；提摩太前书 1:9–11；2:1–8；4:6–8；提摩太后书 3:14–17；希伯来书 12:1–3；13:4–5；雅各书 1:22–27；4:7；彼得前书 2:20–25；3:15；5:8–9；约翰一书 1:3, 6–9；2:15–17。

📖 Scripture References

Psalm 1; 119; Amos 5:24; Matthew 5-7; 18:15-20; Mark 8:34-38; John 8:31-32; 13:34-35; 15:14-15; Acts 2:41-47; Romans 1:24-32; 8:1-30; 12; 1 Corinthians 6:9-11; 11:1; 12:1-13; 2 Corinthians 8-9; Galatians 2:20; 5:16-26; 6:1-2; Ephesians 4:11-12, 15-16; 5:1, 18; Philippians 2:6-8; Colossians 3:1-17; 1 Thessalonians 4:3-8; 5:17; 1 Timothy 1:9-11; 2:1-8; 4:6-8; 2 Timothy 3:14-17; Hebrews 12:1-3; 13:4-5; James 1:22-27; 4:7; 1 Peter 2:20-25; 3:15; 5:8-9; 1 John 1:3, 6-9; 2:15-17.

神学注释 二、神学研读注释
Theological Commentary Commentary
🇨🇳 中文神学注释
🇬🇧 English Commentary

耶稣曾吩咐门徒说：“所以，你们要去，使万民作我的门徒，奉父、子、圣灵的名给他们施洗……凡我所吩咐你们的，都教训他们遵守”（马太福音 28:19-20）。我们将这称为“大使命”。耶稣呼召人们效法祂一生对上帝的全然委身。门徒生涯（Discipleship）包含意志的决断行动，即立定心志按照圣经的教导，作为基督的跟从者度日。门徒生涯具体涵盖四个核心维度：在个人层面对耶稣全然委身以跟从祂、与其他信徒联结在独特的共同体中、藉由属灵操练在基督里渐长成熟，并通过顺服的生活方式彰显真实的信心。

Jesus commanded the disciples, “Go and make disciples of all nations, baptizing them…and teaching them to obey everything I have commanded you” (Matthew 28:19-20). We call this the Great Commission. Jesus called for people to imitate his lifelong commitment to God. Discipleship involves the decisive act of the will to commit oneself to live as a follower of Christ as the Bible teaches. Discipleship involves following Jesus in personal commitment to him, uniting in a distinct community with other believers, growing up into Christ through spiritual disciplines, and demonstrating true faith through an obedient lifestyle.

跟从耶稣（Following Jesus）

门徒生涯始于对耶稣基督作为主和救主的个人信心委身。圣经将这一委身称为“重生”（约翰福音 3:3-8；彼得前书 1:3）。当人们将自己委身于耶稣基督时，他们便领受了永生的恩赐（约翰福音 1:12），并立志照着耶稣的生活方式而活（约翰一书 2:4-6）。真正重生的标志之一，就是渴望顺服耶稣的诫命。

在当代世界中，对于不熟悉基督教的人来说，“门徒生涯”（或“作门徒”）这一词汇可能显得陌生。然而在耶稣传道的时代背景中，跟从师傅或成为门徒的概念却是极为普遍的日常现象。门徒就是委身向宗师求教学艺的学徒门生。耶稣所赋予的门徒之道，不仅包括学习领会祂的教训，更包括藉由效法祂的生命，立志跟从耶稣的脚踪行。在本条中，我们旨在阐明跟从或效法耶稣的具体内涵。

门徒不效法这个世代的样式，而是更新心意与生活，立志讨上帝的喜悦（罗马书 12:1-2）。“现今的世代”（present age）与“上帝统治的新纪元/新世代”（new age）相对立的概念，源自使徒保罗的神学思想。保罗所说的“这世界”，是一个由企图掌控并扭曲人类思想的政治与属灵权势构成的复合体系。保罗谈到了一场争夺人类心智的激烈交锋（哥林多后书 10:4-5）。在他眼中，这是一场在信徒与跟从罪恶国度掌权者的属灵邪恶势力之间的争战（以弗所书 2:1-3；6:10-18）。现今的世代与新世代形成鲜明对比，后者乃是上帝掌权之国度的突破性降临（inbreaking reign of God）；这一国度已经开启（哥林多后书 5:17），但尚未完全显明（处于“已然未然”的救赎张力之中）。

在上帝掌权的这一新纪元中，门徒的鲜明标记是仆人式的生活方式。服事既涵盖敬拜上帝，也涵盖满足人类的需要。真正的虔诚既包括赞美上帝，也包括服事同胞邻舍（希伯来书 13:15-16；雅各书 1:27）。

所有信徒都受了圣灵的洗（哥林多前书 12:13）。所有信徒都领受了圣灵（罗马书 8:9），并且所有人都蒙召要持续不断地被圣灵充满（以弗所书 5:18）。被圣灵充满，实际上等同于顺服基督的诫命。被圣灵充满的基督徒有责任活出上帝儿女的样式（罗马书 8:12-17）。保罗使用了脱去罪恶的生活方式、穿上公义行为的比喻（以弗所书 4:17–5:21；歌罗西书 3:5-17）。他也提出了将旧人治死、穿上新生命的比喻（罗马书 6:1-4）。

Following Jesus

Discipleship begins with a personal commitment of faith in Jesus Christ as Lord and Saviour. The Bible refers to this commitment as the new birth ( John 3:3-8; 1 Peter 1:3). When people commit themselves to Jesus Christ, they receive the gift of eternal life ( John 1:12) and they commit themselves to live as Jesus did (1 John 2:4-6). One of the marks of genuine new birth is the desire to obey the commands of Jesus.

In the contemporary world, the term “discipleship” may be foreign to those unfamiliar with Christianity. In the world in which Jesus ministered, the concept of following a master, or becoming a disciple, was commonplace. Disciples were students who committed themselves to learning from a master teacher. The discipleship Jesus offered involved not only learning his teachings but also committing to follow Jesus by imitating his life. In this article, we seek to describe what following, or imitating, Jesus means.

Disciples do not conform themselves to the pattern of the present age but have their lives and mindsets transformed in their aim to please God (Romans 12:1-2). The concept of a present age as opposed to the new age in which God rules is Pauline. Paul writes of “this world” as a complex of political and spiritual powers seeking to control and warp human thinking. Paul speaks of a battle for the minds of humanity (2 Corinthians 10:4-5). He envisions a battle between the believers and the spiritual evil forces that follow the ruler of the sinful kingdom (Ephesians 2:1-3; 6:10-18). The present age is contrasted with the new age, the inbreaking reign of God which has already begun (2 Corinthians 5:17) but has not yet been fully revealed. In this new era of God’s reign, disciples are marked by a servant lifestyle. Service involves both worshipping God and meeting human need. True religion involves both praising God and serving fellow humans (Hebrews 13:15-16; James 1:27).

All believers are baptized by the Holy Spirit (1 Corinthians 12:13). All believers have received the Holy Spirit (Romans 8:9), and all are called to be continually filled with the Spirit (Ephesians 5:18). Spirit-filling is the equivalent of being obedient to the commands of Christ. Spirit-filled Christians have an obligation to live as God’s children (Romans 8:12-17). Paul uses the image of stripping off the sinful lifestyle and being clothed with righteous deeds (Ephesians 4:17-5:21; Colossians 3:5-17). The image of putting to death the old self and putting on new life is also suggested (Romans 6:1-4).

联结于独特的共同体（United in a Distinct Community）

门徒生涯绝非遗世独立的孤旅。当耶稣最初呼召门徒时，那不仅是呼召他们个人跟从祂，更是呼召他们加入一个门徒跟从者的共同体。在崇尚极端个人主义（rugged individualism）却又深感个人孤立无援与软弱无力的当代文化中，走向共同体的呼召带来了真实的盼望。圣经教导我们，共同体是困乏艰难之人的支持与鼓励之源（加拉太书 6:1-2）。在共同体中，信徒应当彼此守望问责、互相担当负责（马太福音 5:23-26；18:15-20）。

我们教导，洗礼不仅是个人在基督里得着新生命的见证，更是一项加入基督身体（具体彰显于地方教会堂会会众中）的圣约委身（哥林多前书 12:13；使徒行传 2:41-47）。藉由洗礼与成为教会会友，门徒确认了自己建立基督身体的使命与职责（以弗所书 4:16）。每位信徒都蒙召为基督作见证（使徒行传 1:8）；与此同时，教会作为一个群体，也借着其宣讲与生活方式共同向世人作见证。

United in a Distinct Community

Discipleship is not a solitary lifestyle. When Jesus called the initial disciples, it was a call not just to follow him but to join a community of followers. In the contemporary culture which values rugged individualism but recognizes personal inadequacy, the call to community offers hope. The Scriptures teach that the community is a source of support and encouragement for those in need (Galatians 6:1-2). In the community, believers are to be accountable to and for one another (Matthew 5:23-26; 18:15-20).

We teach that baptism is not only a personal witness of new life in Christ but also a commitment to join Christ’s body as expressed in the local congregation (1 Corinthians 12:13; Acts 2:41-47). Through baptism and church membership, disciples acknowledge their task to build up Christ’s body (Ephesians 4:16). Each individual is called to witness for Christ (Acts 1:8). At the same time, the church witnesses corporately through its proclamation and lifestyle.

在基督里长进（Growing Up into Christ）

作门徒是在知行合一的实践中学会的。基督徒借着操练经典的属灵操练（灵性操练），在信心中扎根成长。圣经是信徒信仰与行事为人不可动摇的指南。耶稣的跟从者当留心听从圣经的训诲，研读、默想并背诵上帝的话语（约书亚记 1:8；诗篇 1:2；19:7-14；119:9, 11, 105；提摩太后书 2:15；3:14-17）。

在与敌对基督的世俗体系的冲突中，祷告是保护并坚固信徒的得胜利器。基督徒藉由祷告与上帝相交交通。祷告与禁食为信徒抵挡魔鬼权势的属灵争战赐下力量（马可福音 9:29；以弗所书 6:10-18），并成为圣灵引导教会前行道路的关键途径（使徒行传 13:1-3）。信徒在祷告中向上帝祈求作忠心见证人的勇气（使徒行传 4:29-30；歌罗西书 4:4）。祷告使人得享与上帝的深契相交，并在受试炼的艰难时刻赐下安慰。

在当代教会中，认罪在很大程度上演变成了一项纯粹私人的行为。从历史上看，这种“私人化”的成因至少有两方面：其一，宗教改革时期的抗罗宗（新教）为反对中世纪罗马天主教会告解礼（confessional rites）的弊端，彻底取缔了这一群体仪式；其二，虽然彼此认罪是早期再洗礼派（Anabaptists）的鲜明特征，但后来过于律法主义式的“绝罚/禁绝交往”（banning）以及其他极端的教会纪律形式，也促成了当今认罪走向极端私人化的局面。然而，圣经的教导十分清晰明白：信徒应当“彼此认罪，互相代求”（雅各书 5:16）。

在共同体内部操练分享资源，也是门徒的显著特质。肢体当善用各自的属灵恩赐来建立基督的身体（罗马书 12:3-8；哥林多前书 12:7）；当按着缺乏者的实际需要分享物质资源（申命记 15:7-11；哥林多后书 8:13-15）。

圣经教导我们：成长既是上帝白白的恩赐，也是个人与群体操练的果实。这二者的并置交织，在腓立比书 2:12-13 中体现得最为透彻。从人的角度来看，在基督里的成长需要付诸努力——具体而言，就是对顺服的呼召作出回应；而从上帝的角度来看，成长是一份恩典的赏赐——是上帝亲自定意并在信徒心里运行作成的美工。

Growing Up into Christ

Discipleship is learned by doing. Christians grow in their faith by practicing the classical spiritual disciplines. The Bible is the believers’ guide for faith and practice. Followers of Jesus heed the biblical admonition to read, meditate on, and memorize the Word ( Joshua 1:8; Psalm 1:2; 19:7-14; 119:9, 11, 105; 2 Timothy 2:15; 3:14-17).

In the conflict with the anti-christian world system, prayer is the weapon that protects and fortifies the believer. Through prayer, the Christian communicates with God. Prayer and fasting give strength for the battle with the demonic world (Mark 9:29; Ephesians 6:10-18) and become the means by which the Spirit directs the church (Acts 13:1-3). In prayer, believers ask God for the courage to be faithful witnesses (Acts 4:29-30; Colossians 4:4). Prayer offers communion with God and comfort in times of testing. Confession of sin has largely become a private act in the contemporary church. Historically, the reasons for this privatization are at least twofold. First, Protestants reacted against the abuses of the confessional rites of the medieval Roman Catholic Church by eliminating the practice altogether. Second, although confession of sins characterized early Anabaptists, an excessively legalistic practice of banning and other extreme forms of church discipline have also contributed to the present situation. Biblical teaching is clear, however, that believers should confess their sins one to another ( James 5:16).

Disciples are also characterized by the discipline of sharing resources within the community. Members are to use their spiritual gifts to build up the body (Romans 12:3-8; 1 Corinthians 12:7). Material resources are to be shared as there is need (Deuteronomy 15:7-11; 2 Corinthians 8:13-15). The Scriptures teach that growth is God’s gift and also that growth results from personal and corporate disciplines. Nowhere is the juxtaposition clearer than in Philippians 2:12-13. From the human perspective, growth in Christ involves work—specifically, the call to obedience. From God’s viewpoint, growth is a gift—something that God purposes and produces.

彰显真实的信心（Demonstrating True Faith）

新约圣经毫无疑问地显明，两种针锋相对的体系正在争夺人类的灵魂。加拉太书 5:16-26 不仅列举了相互对立的生命特征清单，更生动描绘了圣灵与肉体情欲（罪恶本性）之间的激烈交战。在约翰一书 2:15-17 中，我们读到爱世界的心与爱父的心是势不两立的。在一个推崇多元主义与包容宽容的世界中，圣经关于两大体系冲突的信息听起来或许有些“不合时宜”；然而，今日的冲突即便有时更加隐蔽、不易察觉，这场争战在我们的时代却实则更为激烈残酷。

对耶稣而言，作门徒的本质在于向自我死（对己死，舍己，马可福音 8:34-38）。耶稣深知自己的使命必然不可避免地引向十字架上的受死。作为基督徒，我们确信耶稣的死具有无可替代、永不可重复的代赎性特质。罗马书 5:6-11 教导我们，基督是“为我们死”。

耶稣同样教导说，祂对自我的舍弃是门徒当效法的典范。从定义上讲，“向自我死”必然要求舍己。舍己包含一种放弃坚持个人自辩伸冤权利的心态。背负十字架，则是无论付出何等代价，都将这种心态付诸顺服耶稣的实际行动。对历代与当今的许多信徒而言，背负十字架曾经意味着、如今也依然意味着遭受逼迫与殉道。承认并接受基督的主权，就是将我们生命的掌控权从“自我”的手中挪开，全然交托在上主的主权慈爱之中。作为基督的仆人（甚至“奴仆”），我们蒙召跟从耶稣直至受死，毫无保留，不顾个人的安逸与安危。

第 10 条列举了若干对比鲜明的陈述，从实际生活的角度具体刻画了“向自我死”的真谛：门徒拒绝物质主义（拜金主义），作财务资源的忠心管家（见第 15 条）；门徒拒绝暴力，践行基督徒爱与非抵抗的崇高呼召（见第 13 条）；门徒拒绝欺诈虚谎的行径，蒙召藉由言语诚实以及在商业贸易中秉公行事来彰显诚信正直（见第 12 条）；门徒拒绝不道德的行为，立志持守性纯洁（见第 11 条）。

总而言之，跟从基督必须在实际生命中彰显出来。历代教会都在见证这一真理。当年有一位信徒为了继续从事异教行业而辩护，宣称“我总得活下去啊”，早期教父特土良（Tertullian）反问道：“难道你非活不可吗？”再洗礼派改革先驱汉斯·登克（Hans Denck）曾留下名言：“若非在生活中跟从基督，无人能真正认识祂。”在二十世纪，德国神学家迪特里希·朋霍费尔（Dietrich Bonhoeffer）在其名作《追随基督》中写道：“十字架摆在每一个基督徒面前……当基督呼召一个人时，祂是吩咐他来，为祂而死。”

Demonstrating True Faith

The New Testament leaves little doubt that two antagonistic systems are at war for humankind. Galatians 5:16-26 not only provides a list of competing characteristics but also offers the image of conflict between the Spirit and the sinful nature. In 1 John 2:15-17, we read that love for the world is antithetical to loving God. In a world of pluralism and tolerance, the biblical message of conflicting systems may sound outdated. The conflict may at times be more insidious and less obvious, but the battle is, if anything, even fiercer in our day.

For Jesus, the essence of discipleship involved death to self (Mark 8:3438). Jesus himself understood his mission as one which was leading inevitably to death on the cross. As Christians, we understand that Jesus’ death had an unrepeatable, substitutionary character. Romans 5:6-11 teaches that Christ died for us.

Jesus also taught that his death to self was a model for disciples to follow. We understand that death to self by definition demands self-denial. Selfdenial involves an attitude that surrenders the right of insisting on personal vindication. Cross-bearing puts that attitude into acts of obedience to Jesus despite any cost. For many believers, cross-bearing has meant and continues to mean persecution and martyrdom. To accept Christ’s lordship removes control of our lives from self and places it in the sovereign love of God. As slaves to Christ, we are called to follow Jesus to death without regard for personal comfort or safety.

Article 10 lists several antithetical statements that describe death to self in practical terms. Disciples reject materialism and are stewards of financial resources (see Article 15). They reject violence and follow the Christian call to love and nonresistance (see Article 13). Disciples reject dishonest behaviour and are called to show integrity by speaking honestly and by doing business in a fair manner (see Article 12). Disciples reject immorality and commit to be sexually pure (see Article 11).

In summary, following Christ must be demonstrated in life. The church has expressed this notion throughout the ages. When confronted by a believer who argued in favour of continuing his pagan trade because he must live, the church father Tertullian asked, “Must you?” The Anabaptist reformer Hans Denk said, “To know Christ one must follow him in life.” In the 20th century Dietrich Bonhoffer wrote, “The cross is laid on every Christian… When Christ calls persons, he bids them come and die.”

教牧应用 三、教牧实践应用
Pastoral Application Pastoral Application
🇨🇳 中文教牧应用
🇬🇧 English Pastoral Application

作门徒是所有信徒的普遍常态。门徒生涯意味着每位信徒都蒙召去跟从、学习并效法基督。对基督的委身敬虔与属灵成长绝非少数“属灵精英”的特权。教会的使命是接纳处于门徒旅程不同阶段的人，并激励他们向着满有基督长成的身量迈进。

Discipleship is the norm for all believers. Discipleship means that every believer is called to follow, learn from, and imitate Christ. Devotion to Christ and spiritual growth are not only for a special few. Our calling as a church is to accept people at different stages in their discipleship journey and to stimulate them toward Christlikeness.

榜样示范的门徒造就（Discipleship as Modelling）

我们的信仰宣言申明，门徒生涯包含联结于一个独特的共同体——地方教会。这意味着与其他门徒进入彼此立约的圣约关系，而绝非仅仅成为一个机构体制性活动项目的参与者。“效法/模仿”（Imitation）是圣经关于门徒之道教导中的核心概念。上帝的子民蒙召要像上帝一样，效法上帝的品格与作为。主耶稣邀请跟从祂的人“学我的样式”；使徒保罗吩咐基督徒效法他的榜样，如同他效法基督一样。

当成熟的基督徒在初信者（年幼信徒）的属灵旅程中与他们并肩同行时，门徒造就便得以真实发生。行之有效的榜样示范，需要人在一段相当长的时间内保持频繁的深入接触。当信徒一同经历各种人生境遇时，他们便有契机在潜移默化中培养基督徒的态度、价值观与言行举止，并共同探讨指导基督化生活方式的属灵原则。门徒造就还包括学习操练能促进灵命长进的各项属灵纪律，其中包括规律的祷告、查经、默想、与其他基督徒团契相交，以及分享自己的信仰见证。良师益友的导师关系（mentoring relationship）为这些操练中的彼此守望问责提供了具体的成长场景。当堂会会众有意识、有计划地推动这种门徒导师关系时，整个教会都必得坚固。

基督徒积极正面的榜样示范，也应当在血缘家庭中扎根实践。教会应当高度重视保护与滋养家庭，并激励家庭在日常生活中活出始终如一的门徒见证。与此同时，教会应当避免越俎代庖，替家庭承担本属于家庭自身的属灵职责。教导父母深爱子女，并勇于承担在信仰中养育教导儿女的责任，乃是在教会内部培育属灵健康的人格与建立健全人际关系的关键所在。

当今文化盛行的氛围过度鼓吹个人独立与极端个人主义。为了抵御这一世俗风气，许多教会正在大教会家庭内部大力发展小组团契。小组为深化肢体关系、促进属灵成长、满足特殊关怀与支持需求、在安全信任的氛围中持守彼此问责，以及提供操练和磨砺事奉技能的机会，提供了一个极为切实的途径。

发展形式多样的小组与事工团队，有助于满足教会家庭内部的多元需求，且每一个群组都能成为门徒造就的有效平台。即便是事务导向（事工性）的小组，只要他们在处理事务的过程中注重建立肢体关系，也能化为门徒训练的宝贵契机。教会群体更应当乐于坦诚分享：自己的信仰是如何切实影响并指导日常所作出的各项决定的。

Discipleship as Modelling

Our confession asserts that discipleship involves being united in a distinct community, a local church. This means entering into a covenant relationship with other disciples, not simply becoming part of an institutional program. Imitation is a key concept in the biblical teaching of discipleship. God’s people are called to be like God, to imitate the character and actions of God. Jesus invites his followers to “learn from him.” The apostle Paul tells Christians to follow his example as he followed Christ.

Discipleship happens when mature Christians walk with younger believers in their spiritual journey. Effective modelling requires frequent contact between people over an extended period of time. As believers experience a variety of life situations together, they have opportunities to develop Christian attitudes, values, and actions, and to discuss the principles that guide a Christlike lifestyle. Discipleship also involves learning to practice disciplines that lead to spiritual growth. These include regular prayer, Bible study, meditation, fellowship with other Christians, and sharing of one’s faith. A mentoring relationship provides a context for mutual accountability in the practice of these disciplines. Congregations are strengthened when they are intentional about facilitating discipling relationships. Positive Christian modelling should also take place within the biological family. A church should place high priority on protecting and nurturing families and challenging them to consistent discipleship. At the same time, a church should avoid doing for families what they should do for themselves. Instructing parents to love their children and accept responsibility for educating them in the faith is a key to building spiritually healthy people and relationships within the church The prevailing mood of our culture encourages personal independence and individualism. To counter this, many churches are developing small groups within the larger church family. They offer a viable context for deepening relationships, growing spiritually, meeting special support needs, maintaining accountability in a secure setting, and providing opportunities to sharpen ministry skills.

Developing a variety of small groups and ministry teams helps meet the diverse needs within a church family, with each group providing a context for discipleship. Even task-oriented groups become opportunities for discipleship when they seek to build relationships as they conduct business. Church groups need to share more readily how their faith impacts the decisions they make.

门徒生涯与守望问责（Discipleship and Accountability）

作门徒必然意味着在共同体中彼此守望问责。这种问责可以在多个层面上实践。一对一的相交关系与小组团契能够为彼此鼓励、挑战、劝戒与纠正提供极佳的土壤。新约圣经教导我们，每位信徒的态度与行为都会直接影响整个基督身体的生命活力。教会的会众纪律（Congregational discipline）旨在服事并挽回教会中那些深陷罪恶捆绑的人。执行纪律绝不可出于敌意、冷酷或排斥的心态，而必须始终本着基督的爱来施行，其唯一的宗旨乃是促使人知罪、悔改并得着全然的修复与挽回。必须格外审慎地以对相关经文和上帝所设定的教会纪律法则的清晰理解，来落实这种群体的守望问责。与此同时，教会领袖与会众也应当充分了解此类纪律问责在当代公民社会中可能涉及的各项法律问题。

Discipleship and Accountability

Discipleship implies accountability within a community. This accountability can be practiced on several levels. One-to-one relationships and small groups can provide a context for encouragement, challenge, reproof, and correction. The New Testament teaches that the attitudes and actions of each believer affect the life of the entire body. Congregational discipline ministers to those in the church that are in bondage to sin. Discipline must never be carried out in a spirit of hostility but should be administered in love with the desire to produce conviction, repentance, and restoration. Care must be taken to practice corporate accountability with a clear understanding of relevant Scripture and God’s pattern for church discipline. At the same time, leaders and congregations should inform themselves of the various legal issues that this type of accountability may involve.

门徒生涯与从世界分别（Discipleship and Separation from the World）

主耶稣吩咐跟从祂的人要“在世，却不属世”。然而，有些人将这种“分别”（separation）误解为文化上的自我隔绝和对特定文化活动的一味回避。他们将作门徒与一种刻板僵化的生活方式划等号——似乎生活越苦修严苛，就越显得属灵敬虔。我们的信仰宣言则旨在将“不效法世界”（non-conformity）阐释为一种积极的呼召，即从世俗不敬虔的价值观与邪恶败坏的行径中分别出来。当门徒将自己全然奉献给上帝，使自己的价值观与上帝的价值观相吻合、使自己的生命与上帝的救赎旨意相一致时，他们就被分别出来归主为圣了。

门徒虽从恶行中分别出来，但绝不可与爱世人的实际需要相隔绝。信徒蒙召在这个迷失的世界中作盐作光。肩负救赎使命的敏锐门徒，必然生活在一种张力之中：既要在社会群体中发挥积极的感化力，又要面对并兼顾其他信徒的期望与顾虑。在当年的宗教律法主义者眼中，耶稣出入的无疑是“错误的地方”，交往的也是“错误的人”。然而，耶稣既没有姑息纵容罪人的罪性，也没有任凭罪人继续滞留在声名狼藉的罪恶泥潭之中。

耶稣所祈求的，不是天父将门徒从这世界上撤离，而是保守他们脱离那恶者。这需要极大的牧养智慧与属灵敏锐度，来教导门徒远离邪恶本身，但不必切断与那些深受恶者摧残辖制之受害者的一切联系与关怀。

Discipleship and Separation from the World

Jesus instructed his followers to be in the world but not of the world. Some have understood this separation to mean cultural isolation and the avoidance of particular cultural activities. They associate discipleship with a rigid lifestyle—the more austere, the more godly. Our confessional statement seeks to present non-conformity as a call to separation from the godless values and evil practices in the world. Disciples are set apart as they dedicate themselves to God, aligning their values to God’s values and their lives to God’s purposes.

Disciples separate themselves from evil practices, but they can never separate themselves from the practical need to love people. Believers are called to be salt and light in a lost world. The perceptive disciple on a redemptive mission will live with the tension between influencing the community and satisfying the expectations of other believers. Jesus certainly frequented the wrong places and met with the wrong people according to the religious legalists. Yet he neither condoned a sinner’s sinfulness nor left the sinners to continue in their notorious ways.

Jesus prayed not that the Father would take his disciples out of the world but that He would protect them from the evil one. Great sensitivity is needed to teach disciples to avoid evil but not necessarily every association with victims of the evil one.

日常生活中的门徒实践（Discipleship in Everyday Life）

耶稣教导说，门徒之路乃是舍己之路。跟从者背起十字架，就是在人生的各种处境中有意识地定意去代表基督、活出基督。他们选择尊重其他信徒为在基督里同蒙创造与救赎的肢体；他们乐意为他人的益处付出时间与关怀，并为他人的福祉慷慨解囊；他们存着尊重之心对待其他信仰的人，并深切体认到：能够分享自己的时间、金钱与财物来广传福音并周济困苦穷乏之人，乃是一项无比宝贵的恩典与特权。

门徒学习说造就人、鼓励人的恩惠言语，远离有害伤人的话语。当他们在令人不快的棘手处境中必须说出真理实情时，他们会存着温柔与爱心来说。他们立定心志为人诚实，在商业交往以及面对政府部门时杜绝闪烁其词、推诿遮掩。当发生人际冲突时，门徒全力致力于促进和好与和睦。

新约圣经教导信徒不可为了解决冤屈纠纷而将其他信徒告上法庭。在过去，一些门诺弟兄会信徒曾将这些教训一刀切地理解为基督徒绝不可出庭打官司。经过更深入的研察，我们看到基督徒之所以不可彼此对簿公堂，是因为在公义的审断上，争端双方本应顺服于同一位至高权威——上帝。然而，当面临必须纠正的不公义事件，而另一方并不顺服上帝的法则与权威时，圣经对于当如何行并未作硬性规定（保持了沉默）。在现实生活中，信徒也屡屡被传唤出庭作证或应诉。

教牧人员需要懂得如何就诉诸法庭的问题提供适切的牧养辅导。我们确信，基督徒门徒当维护我们社会中的司法公义体系，包括在确有必要时与非信徒对簿公堂——只要这样做是为了社会的公共福祉与正义，而非出于个人的报复。

信徒在人生与教会生活中不可避免地会遇到冲突。他们必须在彼此相爱的同时，寻找既不破坏人际关系、又不阻碍上帝对教会心意旨意的解决之道。在社会当中，基督徒必须主动采取行动捍卫弱势群体、为遭受不公者仗义执言，并致力于寻求化解争端、促进和解的方案。

门徒在日常生活中跟从耶稣所作出的某些抉择，往往会使个人的计划、期望、抱负和切身利益付出极大的代价。然而，当信徒在作门徒的道路上不断长进时，他们对上帝的爱也日益深厚，并经历到与主耶稣之间无比亲密的相爱——这如同“觅得重价的珍珠”。当他们藉由基督释放人的救赎大工以及圣灵使人成圣的更新作为，被塑造成身量成熟的门徒时，他们便深知何为真正的喜乐、平安与生命的丰盛满足。

Discipleship in Everyday Life

Jesus teaches that discipleship is the way of self-denial. As his followers take up the cross, they consciously decide to represent Christ in the various situations of life. They choose to respect other believers as fellow creations in Christ. They give time and attention to others’ interests and money for their welfare. They treat people of other faiths with respect and recognize that it is a privilege to share time, money, and possessions to help spread the gospel and to help the needy.

Disciples learn to speak edifying and encouraging words, avoiding harmful talk. When they are required to speak the truth in unpleasant situations, they do so gently and in love. They determine to be honest and to eliminate evasiveness in business and with government. When personal conflicts occur, disciples work toward reconciliation.

The New Testament instructs believers not to take other believers to court to settle a wrong. In the past, some Mennonite Brethren have taken these instructions to mean that a Christian should never go to court. On closer examination, we see that Christians do not take each other to court because both parties submit to one and the same authority, God, in matters of justice. However, when there is an injustice to be addressed and the other party does not submit to the laws of God, the Bible remains silent on how to proceed. Frequently, believers are asked to make court appearances.

Pastors need to know how to give counsel concerning the use of courts. We believe that the Christian disciple upholds the justice system in our society, including going to court with non-believers if necessary, as long as it is for the good of society and not for personal revenge.

Believers will face inevitable conflicts. They must continue to love each other while finding solutions that are not destructive to interpersonal relationships nor to God’s purposes for their congregation. In society, Christians must take initiative to defend the powerless, speak out against injustice, and work toward solutions which reconcile those in conflict.

Some of the decisions disciples make as they follow Jesus in everyday life come at great cost to personal plans, hopes, ambition, and self-interest. As believers grow in discipleship, they also grow in their love for God and experience an intimacy and love with Jesus, which is like “finding the pearl of great price.” They know joy, peace, and fulfillment as they are transformed into mature disciples by the liberating work of Christ and the sanctifying work of the Holy Spirit.

ARTICLE 11 门诺弟兄会信仰宣言 · 中英双语对照典藏本

第 11 条：婚姻、单身与家庭 (Marriage, Singleness, and Family)

Article 11: Marriage, Singleness, and Family

信条条文 一、信仰宣言条文
Confession of Faith The Confession of Faith
🇨🇳 中文信条条文
🇬🇧 Original Confession Text

我们相信，婚姻与家庭皆由上帝所设立。教会既祝福婚姻，亦肯定并祝福单身，并鼓励家庭在爱中不断成长。

Marriage, Singleness, and Family

We believe that marriage and the family are instituted by God. The church blesses both marriage and singleness and encourages families to grow in love.

婚姻 (Marriage)

婚姻是一种圣约关系，旨在使一男一女一生结合。在创造之初，上帝设计婚姻是为了让人彼此为伴、在性方面合而为一，以及生养并抚育儿女。性亲密唯有在婚姻之内才具有正当性。婚姻的特质当体现为彼此相爱、忠诚与顺服。信徒不应与非信徒结合。

信仰共同体祝福并维系婚姻关系，并竭尽全力促使陷入危机的婚姻重归于好。然而，人性的罪性有时会导致离婚，这是对上帝设立婚姻原本心意的违背。上帝的家当本着真理与怜悯，在不断持守合乎圣经的婚姻忠贞理想的同时，为经历破碎者带来盼望与医治。

Marriage

Marriage is a covenant relationship intended to unite a man and a woman for life. At creation, God designed marriage for companionship, sexual union, and the birth and nurture of children. Sexual intimacy rightfully takes place only within marriage. Marriage is to be characterized by mutual love, faithfulness, and submission. A believer should not marry an unbeliever. The community of faith blesses and nurtures marriage relationships, and makes every effort to bring reconciliation to troubled marriages. Human sinfulness, however, may sometimes lead to divorce, a violation of God’s intention for marriage. With truth and compassion, the family of God offers hope and healing while continually upholding the biblical ideal of marital faithfulness.

单身 (Singleness)

单身与婚姻同享尊荣，在某些情况下甚至更为可取。教会应当祝福、尊重并完全接纳单身人士。保持单身者可以享有独特的契机以拓展上帝的国度。上帝呼召所有人——无论是单身还是已婚——都要过圣洁贞洁的生活。

Singleness

Singleness is honoured equally with marriage, sometimes even preferred. The church is to bless, respect, and fully include those who are single. Those who remain single may find unique opportunities to advance the kingdom of God. God calls all people, single and married, to live sexually pure lives.

家庭 (Family)

上帝的心意是让人在生命各个阶段的家庭关系中都充满爱。儿女是上帝所赐的产业与恩赐。敬虔的父母应当在信仰中教导并抚养儿女。父母当以智慧和爱心管教儿女，不可惹儿女的气。儿女应当孝敬并顺从父母。

Family

God intends family relationships at all stages of life to be characterized by love. Children are a gift from God. Godly parents instruct and nurture their children in the faith. Parents are to discipline their children wisely and lovingly, not provoking them to anger. Children are to honour and obey their parents.

📖 经文引证 (和合本 CUV)

创世记 1:26-31；2:18-24；5:1-2；12:1-3；出埃及记 22:16-17；利未记 18:22；20:13；申命记 6:4；24:1-4；诗篇 127:3-5；箴言 31章；马太福音 5:32；10:34-39；19:3-12；22:23-33；马可福音 3:31-35；7:9-13；10:6-11；路加福音 16:18；罗马书 7:2-3；14:12；哥林多前书 7:8-40；哥林多后书 6:14-15；以弗所书 5:21-6:4；提摩太前书 3:1-13；5:3-16；希伯来书 13:4；彼得前书 3:1-7。

📖 Scripture References

Genesis 1:26-31; 2:18-24; 5:1-2; 12:1-3; Exodus 22:16-17; Leviticus 18:22; 20:13; Deuteronomy 6:4; 24:1-4; Psalm 127:3-5; Proverbs 31; Matthew 5:32; 10:34-39; 19:3-12; 22:23-33; Mark 3:31-35; 7:9-13; 10:6-11; Luke 16:18; Romans 7:2-3; 14:12; 1 Corinthians 7:8-40; 2 Corinthians 6:14-15; Ephesians 5:21-6:4; 1 Timothy 3:1-13; 5:3-16; Hebrews 13:4; 1 Peter 3:1-7.

神学注释 二、神学研读注释
Theological Commentary Commentary
🇨🇳 中文神学注释
🇬🇧 English Commentary

引言

圣经教导，婚姻的圣约委身旨在建立一段终生的伴侣关系，并为抚育儿女提供适切的环境。有些人不会结婚，圣经教导单身人士能够处于不受家庭生活纷扰、专心事奉主的位置。圣经禁止离婚，为离婚的发生而哀痛，严格限制再婚，但同时也表明，对经历离婚之人的牧养关怀应当包含饶恕以及恢复团契相交。第11条正是阐述婚姻、离婚、单身与家庭的相关课题。

Introduction

The Bible teaches that the covenant commitment of marriage is for establishing a lifelong relationship which will provide the context for child rearing. Some will not marry, and Scripture teaches that single persons are positioned to serve the Lord without the distractions of family life. The Bible forbids divorce, laments its occurrence, restricts remarriage, but suggests that pastoral care for those who have experienced divorce should include forgiveness and restoration to fellowship. Article 11 deals with the matters of marriage, divorce, singleness, and family.

婚姻 (Marriage)

旧约中的婚姻

上帝创造了具有性别的受造物——男人与女人（创世记 1:27）。创造叙事彰显了造物主对男女的精妙设计。无论男女，人类都被赋予了“要生养众多，遍满地面……”（创世记 1:28）的使命。那人（亚当）独居不好；上帝看明他需要一个相称的帮手作他的伴侣（创世记 2:18）。经文叙事教导：“人要离开父母，与妻子连合，二人成为一体”（创世记 2:24）。在起初这一纯洁无瑕的状态中，人类第一对男女赤身露体，并不觉得羞耻（创世记 2:25）。

这段经文叙事启示了婚姻的多重神圣旨意与基本原则：

  1. 回应人类对亲密相通的渴求：婚姻设立之初，正为回应人类心灵深处对亲密关系的切慕；男女被造，原本就是要成为彼此扶持、休戚与共的终生伴侣。
  2. 孕育生养儿女的神圣土壤：婚姻为繁衍后代设立了正当而蒙福的圣洁保障；生养众多是创造秩序中不可或缺的神圣托付，这秩序曾被上帝亲自称赞为“甚好”（创世记 1:31）。婚姻正是维系家庭的基石。
  3. 确立崭新家庭单元的起点：创世记经文明确教导，人结婚时当“离开父母，与妻子连合”，以此建立独立而崭新的家庭圣约。
  4. 两性身体结合的唯一正当场域：夫妻二人借着身心灵的深度连合，在上帝面前真正成为“一体”。
  5. 蒙受圣洁欢悦与全然释放的神圣赐福：夫妻间的亲密结合深蒙上帝所赐圣洁喜悦的祝福；二人赤身露体并不羞耻，在婚姻的圣约之内得享一种坦荡敞开、毫无顾忌的全然释放与彼此悦纳。
  6. 一生一世、一对一的忠贞独占性委身：尽管在旧约历史中后世圣徒曾出现纳妾多妻的悖离现象，但创造的原初秩序从未认可多妻制度具有任何良善的价值。
  7. 一男一女、异性结合的神圣定则：圣经启示的婚姻本具异性结合的本质。上帝设立的创造秩序断然排除了同性结合的可能，同性结合决不可能承载唯有男女婚姻才配得享有的美善与神圣祝福。

创世记第3章关于堕落的叙事表明，罪玷污了受造界的良善。这种扭曲表现在人类第一对夫妻之间的隔阂与疏离，并且因着罪的咒诅，这种疏离注定会继续困扰后世的夫妻。妻子面临生产的苦楚，以及与配偶关系中的痛苦——其特征表现为妻子恋慕丈夫，而丈夫必管辖她（创世记 3:16）。

律法典章进一步强化了创造的定例。十诫中明确禁止通奸（出埃及记 20:14；申命记 5:18）。圣经中详尽的性道德律例严禁乱伦与同性结合（利未记 18章列举了一系列相关禁令）。摩西律法始终认定：婚姻是一男一女之间终生的圣约。

旧约的历史叙事也揭示了婚姻所面临的复杂困境。亚伯拉罕纳夏甲为妾（代孕）导致了他与撒拉关系的严重恶化，这不仅给撒拉带来了巨大的内心痛苦，更导致了日后以色列人与以实玛利后裔之间的长久敌对与疏离。雅各的妻妾在嫉妒与偏宠中苦苦挣扎。大卫虽是合上帝心意的人，却效法古代近东的专制君王纳了多位妻妾，最终不得不承受家庭内部骨肉相残、纷争不断的恶果。其子所罗门试图通过迎娶一千个妃嫔来巩固其王权，最终导致了严重的属灵堕落。

先知们常常借用婚姻圣约所要求的忠贞，作为一面镜子来指责以色列对耶和华的不忠。何西阿娶淫妇为妻，以此生动体现耶和华在与以色列立约中所经历的背叛与伤痛。玛拉基则严厉谴责丈夫对幼年所娶之妻的不忠与背信（玛拉基书 2:14-16）。

智慧文学——尤其是箴言、传道书与雅歌——讴歌了婚姻的喜乐。箴言将美满幸福的婚姻称为耶和华的恩赐（箴言 12:4；18:22；19:14）。传道书教导人当与所爱的妻快活度日（传道书 9:9）。雅歌则尽情颂扬了亲密关系中身体与感官的欢悦。

新约中的婚姻

当耶稣教导婚姻时，祂将教训扎根于创造的典章与定例之中（马可福音 10:6-9）。耶稣重申了婚姻伴侣的男女两性特质、婚姻结合所达致的合一性，以及圣约委身的恒久性。耶稣始终肯定婚姻是来自上帝的恩赐。

对以弗所书 5:22-33 及歌罗西书 3:18-19 中保罗“家规”（household rules）的最佳诠释，当认识到保罗教导的颠覆性与革命性特征。保罗将妻子视为具有独立道德位格的个体，呼召她顺服自己的丈夫。这一呼召是更广泛的“彼此顺服”（以弗所书 5:21）原则的一部分。该希腊词（hypotasso）源自军事用语，意指家庭成员之间应当以恰当有序的方式彼此配合与对齐。而在以弗所书第5章中，保罗对丈夫的劝诫篇幅约为对妻子的两倍，他呼召丈夫效法基督对待教会的方式来施行作头的权柄。这种对基督之爱的效法，其本质特征是舍己的奉献与牺牲。若丈夫如基督爱教会那般爱妻子，“洗脚”式的服事将成为准则，而非高高在上地苛求服事与顺从。

彼得前书 3:1-7 同样给予了妻子和丈夫教导。使徒警戒妻子不要单以“外面的妆饰”为美，而是要有内在长久温柔、安静的心；同时也告诫丈夫要按情理与妻子同住，敬重配偶为生命之恩的同承受者，免得他们的祷告受阻碍。

Marriage

Marriage in the Old Testament: God created humans as sexual beings, male and female (Genesis 1:27). The creation narrative expresses the Creator’s design for men and women. Humans, male and female, are given the mandate to “be fruitful and multiply, and fill the earth . . .” (Genesis 1:28 NRSV). It was not good for a man (Adam) to be alone; God saw that he needed a helper as his partner (Genesis 2:18). The narrative teaches that “a man leaves his father and his mother and clings to his wife, and they become one flesh” (Genesis 2:24 NRSV). In this pristine state, the first pair was naked, yet unashamed (Genesis 2:25). The story seems to suggest several principles. Marriage has multiple purposes. First, marriage responds to the human need for intimacy. Man and woman were created to be companions. Second, it provides the context for reproduction, a mandate that is part of the creation order that God saw as “very good” (Genesis 1:31). Marriage is the foundation for families. Third, marriage is the context for starting a new family unit. At marriage, the narrative teaches, the man leaves his parents and cleaves to his wife. Fourth, marriage is the context for sexual intercourse. Husband and wife cling to each other as a single flesh. Fifth, intimacy seems to be blessed with a sense of holy mutual delight. There is a frankness, a loosing of restrictions, that is encountered in marital union. Sixth, marriage is a one-to-one commitment. Although later Old Testament saints engaged in polygamy, the creation order does not anticipate that this practice is good. Seventh, marriage is heterosexual. Creation order precludes the possibility that homosexual union will be blessed with the goodness of marriage between a man and woman. The narrative of the fall (Genesis 3) indicates that sin has marred the goodness of creation. The distortions include an alienation between the first marital pair, which is destined by the curse of sin to continue to afflict later couples. The wife faces pain in childbirth and pain in her relationship with her mate, characterized as desire for her husband who would rule over her (Genesis 3:16).

The legal code reinforces the creation mandate. Adultery is prohibited in the Ten Commandments (Exodus 20:14; Deuteronomy 5:18). An extensive catalogue of rules related to sexuality includes prohibition of incest and homosexual unions. (Leviticus 18 contains a series of these rules.) The law assumes that marriage is a covenant between a man and a woman for life. The Old Testament narrative raises issues for marriage. The complications of Abraham’s relationship with Sarah when he takes Hagar as a surrogate include not only the pain that Sarah feels but alienation in later international relations with the children of Ishmael. Jacob’s wives and concubines struggle in the face of jealousy and favouritism. David, the man after God’s own heart, acts like an ancient Near Eastern despot by taking multiple wives and faces the consequences of the resulting rivalries within his family. His son Solomon attempts to establish his royal power by taking 1,000 wives and concubines. The prophets use the demand for loyalty within the marriage covenant as an illustration to confront Israel’s unfaithfulness to Yahweh. Hosea takes a wife of prostitution to demonstrate what Yahweh has experienced in the covenant with Israel. Malachi condemns a husband’s failure to be loyal to the wife of his youth (2:14-16).

The wisdom literature, especially the books of Proverbs, Ecclesiastes, and Song of Solomon, celebrate the joy of marriage. Proverbs calls a happy marriage a gift from Yahweh (12:4; 18:22; 19:14). Ecclesiastes teaches that a man should enjoy life with the wife he loves (9:9). Song of Solomon celebrates the sensual delight of intimacy.

Marriage in the New Testament: When Jesus teaches about marriage, he bases his instruction on the creation mandate (Mark 10:6-9). Jesus cites the maleness and femaleness of the marriage partners, the resulting unity of the marriage union, and the permanence of the covenant commitment. Jesus assumes that marriage is a gift from God.

The best interpretation of Paul’s household rules of Ephesians 5:22-33 and Colossians 3:18-19 recognizes the revolutionary character of Paul’s instruction. Paul addresses the wife as an independent moral individual, calling her to be subject to her husband. This call is part of a larger instruction for mutual submission (Ephesians 5:21). The word comes from military terminology, indicating that there is a proper way for members of the household to align themselves with one another. The husband, who receives about twice as many words of instruction as the wife in Ephesians 5, is called to exercise headship as Christ did for the church. This imitation of Christ’s love is to be characterized by self-giving devotion. If the husband loves the wife as Christ loved the church, footwashing rather than demanding service and obedience will be the rule.

First Peter 3:1-7 also gives instructions to wives and husbands. Wives are warned against seeking beauty in “outward adornment.” Husbands are warned that neglecting their marital vows may hinder their prayers.

离婚 (Divorce)

尽管旧约教导与耶稣基督一致认定离婚违背上帝的心意，但双方也都承认，由于人心的刚硬，离婚在现实中确乎存在。

申命记 24:1-4

在摩西民事律法的这段经文中，阐明了四项重要事实：

  1. 摩西承认离婚发生的现实：当他写道：“人若娶妻以后，见她有什么不合宜的事，不喜悦她，就写休书交在她手中，打发她离开妇家……”（24:1），摩西只是客观承认婚姻有失败的可能。他并未刁难提出离婚的男子，也未就破坏婚姻的神圣性与恒久性而大加谴责。
  2. 希伯来社会中离婚的理由相当宽泛且模糊：申命记经文指的是那妻子“因丈夫见她有什么不合宜的事而不喜悦她”（24:1）。“不合宜的事”（something indecent / 希伯来文 ‘erwat dabar）或许有人认为包括了与他人通奸，但这极不可能，因为通奸在律法中的惩罚是死刑，而非离婚（申命记 22:22）。
  3. 改嫁后的妇人绝不可与前夫复婚：被休的妇人若改嫁他人，日后即便后夫去世，她也绝不可再与前夫复婚（24:2-4）。然而，如果她在领受休书后一直未再婚，则可以与前夫重新结合。律法对此设立限制的理由是：如果她已经改嫁，而后夫休了她或去世，“她是已经玷污了”（24:2-4）。
  4. 不可使应许之地蒙受玷污的警告：摩西引述耶和华的话发出警告：“免得你使耶和华你神所赐为业之地得罪”（24:4）。这一禁令的上下文，正是针对男子在妇人改嫁后又与其复婚的情形。土地的玷污并非因为上帝子民中存在离婚现象——离婚纯粹是人类堕落与悲剧的一部分；相反，玷污乃是发生在一名男子休妻之后，在妇人曾改嫁他人的情况下，前夫又重新娶她为妻。

玛拉基书 2:13-16

虽然先知们常用离婚的意象来象征耶和华与以色列之间破裂的盟约，但唯有玛拉基直接陈明了上帝对离婚的直接感受。在玛拉基书 2:16 中，上帝直截了当地宣告：“休妻的事……是我所恨恶的。”经文的上下文将离婚与强暴/暴力联系在一起。背弃婚约乃是对配偶、儿女以及婚姻体制本身的强暴伤害。

在耶稣的时代，离婚是一个争论极其激烈的议题。当时主要有两大思想学派：以一位极具影响力的拉比命名的沙买学派（School of Shammai），持守保守而严谨的立场，“该学派主张，除非发现妻子有不道德的可耻行为，否则丈夫绝不可休妻”（Beare, The Gospel of Matthew, 第154页）。与之针锋相对的希列学派（School of Hillel）则持有极为宽容的立场，认为男子可以因哪怕最微不足道的小事休妻，“哪怕她做饭烧焦了食物”（Beare, 第154页）。

马太福音 5:31-32

在登山宝训中，耶稣介入了这场争论；正如祂对奸淫的教导一样，祂无可比拟地强化并拔高了道德标准。通过“只是我告诉你们……”这句宣告，耶稣虽未彻底封死处理破裂的所有退路，却呼召人进入一种全新的生活方式。耶稣的言论可以分为三个清晰的部分：

  1. 承认现实却绝不容许草率：耶稣承认离婚在现实社会中确实存在。然而，绝不可轻忽对待离婚。唯一的合法离婚理由是淫乱（porneia）——这是一个颇有争议的词汇，可以界定为“不贞、各种非法的性交行为”（Rienecker, A Linguistic Key to the Greek New Testament, 卷一, 第15页）。
  2. 被休妇人面临陷入奸淫的危险：马太福音 5:32下中“叫她作淫妇了”（causes her to become an adulteress）这句短语在含义上存在一定歧义。我们对此有两种解读方式：

- 第一种解释认为，仅因被丈夫休弃这一举动本身，该妇人便已犯了奸淫。尽管有些学者支持这种解读，但要将“奸淫”罪名强加在一个离了婚、但并未再婚且没有性行为的人身上，在逻辑上是极其难以成立的。

- 第二种解释——或许更为合乎情理——认为这指的是那因 porneia（淫乱）而被休的配偶仍在继续重蹈覆辙。因此，耶稣的话当被理解为对刚经历离婚之人的一种警戒，警惕其陷入放纵放荡的生活方式。

  1. 对再婚性关系的严肃定性：耶稣最后的一句话极为沉重与严肃：“人若娶这被休的妇人，也是犯奸淫了。”旧约希伯来文化是一个高度父权制的社会，妇女在某种意义上被视为男子的所有物。因此，提出离婚是男性的特权，尽管在对观福音的平行经文（马可福音 10:1-12）中也涵盖了女性提出离婚的情形。通过宣告“凡娶这被休的妇人的，也是犯奸淫了”，耶稣很可能是将“被休妇人的再婚视为奸淫——对她自身而言，对她的新丈夫而言，皆是如此”（France, Matthew, 第123页）。因此，这一宣告不分性别，对离婚后的一切性关系作出了同样的道德价值判定。

马可福音 10:1-12 与马太福音 19:1-9

在法利赛人前来试探之后，耶稣在马可福音第10章中再次论及离婚问题：

  1. 耶稣阐明摩西律法并非“命令”人离婚，而是因人心的刚硬而“准许”人休妻。
  2. 耶稣指出，离婚的根源在于人心的“刚硬”。
  3. 耶稣援引创造的定例，吩咐人要持守终生的婚姻忠贞。
  4. 耶稣重申了祂先前的教导，即再婚涉及奸淫。尽管有人将这段经文解读为全面禁止一切再婚，但至少有一点是极其明确的：耶稣教导说，一个已婚之人若为了与另一个更有吸引力的人结合而索取休书，其性质与婚外情通奸同样属于犯奸淫。

在哥林多前书 7:10-11 中，保罗重述了主的命令，并呼召那些离了婚的人应当守素安常（保持不婚独身）或与配偶和好。

哥林多前书 6:12-20

这段经文为讨论增添了极为深刻的圣经洞见：二人之间的性结合绝非单纯的生理行为。保罗运用修辞论证道，在性结合的行为中，一个人（此处特指男人与娼妓）在身体上不可分割地结合为一（6:16）。事实上，正是通过这种性行为，“二人要成为一体”。性结合具有如此强大的联合力量，以至于使徒将其与我们与主的联合相提并论：“但与主联合的，便是与主成为一灵”（6:17）。难怪耶稣承认 porneia（非法的性结合/淫乱）构成了离婚的缘由。这更赋予了保罗警告之声以巨大的分量：“你们要逃避淫行”（6:18）。

再婚的复杂性与牧养关怀

尽管圣经对离异者的再婚发出了严厉警戒，但再婚在信徒群体中已变得相当普遍。本信仰宣言呼召教会为离异者提供“盼望与医治”，从而含蓄地承认离婚与再婚属于牧养层面的切实关怀。第11条的“牧养应用”部分将深入探讨再婚的具体课题。

我们彼此之间必须提醒再婚的错综复杂性。许多人若能避免再婚带来的复杂纠纷而保持单身，实为美事。而那些考虑再婚的人，则必须认真审视并处理导致前段婚姻失败的性格特质与根源、与前配偶的关系，以及与前段婚姻中所生儿女的关系。

Divorce

Although both Old Testament teaching and Jesus agree that divorce contradicts the will of God, both also recognize that, due to human hardness of heart, divorce is a reality.

Deuteronomy 24:1-4: Four important truths are noted in this text of Mosaic civil law. First, Moses accepts the occurrence of divorce. When he writes, in part, “If a man marries a woman who becomes displeasing to him ... and writes a certificate of divorce, gives it to her and sends her from his house...” (24:1), Moses simply acknowledges that marriages fail. He doesn’t harass the man who is filing the divorce, nor does he rail against the violation of the sanctity and permanence of marriage.

Second, the grounds for divorce in Hebrew society were very general and rather ambiguous. The words of the Deuteronomy text refer to the wife “who becomes displeasing to him because he finds something indecent about her...” (24:1). The phrase “something indecent” may have included adultery with another man, but this is highly unlikely because the penalty for adultery was death, not divorce (22:22).

Third, a divorced woman who has married another man may never again marry the first husband even if her second husband has died (24:2-4). She may, however, marry her first husband if she has remained unmarried after the certificate of divorce has been issued. The reason given for this restriction is that, if she has remarried and her second husband has either divorced her or has died, “she has been defiled” (24:2-4).

And fourth, Moses, quoting Yahweh, gives a warning: “Do not bring sin upon the land the LORD your God is giving you as an inheritance” (24:4). The context of this statement is the remarriage of a man to his former wife after she has had a second husband. The defilement of the land is not due to divorce being present within the people of God—this is simply part of human tragedy and fallenness. Rather, defilement occurs when a man, who has divorced his wife, remarries her after she, in the intervening period, has been married to another man.

Malachi 2:13-16: Although the prophets use the image of divorce to indicate the broken covenant between Yahweh and Israel, it is Malachi who addresses God’s response to divorce. In Malachi 2:16, God says simply, “I hate divorce.” The context links divorce to violence. The broken covenant is an act of violence against the spouse, the children, and the institution itself.

In Jesus’ day, the matter of divorce was hotly discussed. There were two schools of thought on the subject. The school of Shammai, named after a very influential rabbi, was conservative and rigorous in its position. “It maintained that man ‘must not divorce his wife unless he has found her in a matter of shame’” (Beare, The Gospel of Matthew, 154). The rival school of Hillel held a much more permissible position. A man could divorce her for the merest trifle, “even if she burnt his food in cooking it” (Beare, 154). Matthew 5:31-32: In the Sermon on the Mount, Jesus enters the debate and, as with adultery, strengthens and raises the standard immeasurably. With the phrase “But I tell you...,” Jesus, while not slamming the door on divorce, calls for a new way of living. The words of Jesus can be divided into three distinct parts. First, Jesus concedes that divorce is a reality in society. Divorce, however, must never be trivialized. The only legitimate grounds for divorce is porneia, a debated term which may be defined as “unchastity, fornication of every kind of unlawful sexual intercourse” (Rienecker, A Linguistic Key to the Greek New Testament, Vol. I. p. 15).

Second, the divorced woman is at risk to commit adultery (5:32b). The clause, “causes her to become an adulteress” is somewhat ambiguous in meaning. We can read this clause two ways. First, the words have been taken to mean that in the very act of having been divorced by her husband, the woman now commits adultery. Although some scholars support such a reading, it is very difficult to pin “adultery” on a person who is divorced but who has either not remarried or who is not sexually active. Second, we can interpret this clause to mean, perhaps more logically, that the divorced spouse who has been divorced for porneia is continuing in her ways. The words of Jesus then are to be taken as a caution against the newly divorced person living in a reckless, promiscuous manner.

The final word of Jesus is very troublesome: that is, whoever marries a divorced woman commits adultery. Old Testament Hebrew culture was a strongly patriarchal society. Women were regarded as the property of men. Therefore, it was the prerogative of men to initiate divorce procedures, although the parallel synoptic passage also includes women in this role (Mark 10:1-12). By saying “anyone who marries the divorced woman commits adultery,” Jesus likely regarded “the remarriage of the divorced woman as adultery both on her part and on the part of her new husband” (France, Matthew, 123). Therefore the statement places the same value judgment on all post-divorce sexual liaisons regardless of gender.

Mark 10:1-12 (Matthew 19:1-9): Jesus revisits the question of divorce in Mark 10 after some Pharisees question him. First, Jesus clarifies the Mosaic law as not commanding but allowing divorce. Second, Jesus attributes divorce to hardness of heart. Third, Jesus uses the creation mandate to enjoin lifelong marital faithfulness. Fourth, Jesus reiterates his earlier teaching that remarriage involves adultery. Although one may read the text to prohibit all remarriage, it is at least clear that Jesus is teaching that for a married person to get a writ of divorce for the purpose of marrying a more appealing person is as adulterous as an affair. In 1 Corinthians 7:10-11, Paul repeats the Lord’s command and calls for celibacy for the divorced.

1 Corinthians 6:12-20: What this text adds to the discussion is the profound biblical understanding that sexual union between two persons is more than simply sex. In the act of sexual union, Paul argues rhetorically, a person (in this case, a man and a prostitute) are inseparably united as one “with her in body” (6:16). In fact, through this sexual act, “the two will become one flesh.” The act of sexual union is such a powerful bonding force that it is likened to our union with the Lord: “But he who unites himself with the Lord is one with him in spirit” (6:17). Little wonder that Jesus concedes that porneia, illicit sexual union, is grounds for divorce. This adds power to Paul’s cry of warning: “Flee from sexual immorality” (6:18).

Although Scripture warns against remarriage for divorced persons, remarriage has become rather common among believers. The confession of faith implicitly recognizes that divorce and remarriage is a pastoral concern by calling the church to offer “hope and healing” to the divorced. The pastoral application of Article 11 deals with the issues of remarriage.

We need to caution one another about the complexity of remarriage. Many will do well to avoid the complications of remarriage and remain single. Those who contemplate remarriage must address issues of the characteristics which led to marital failure, relations with former spouses, and relations with children from earlier marriages.

单身 (Singleness)

创造叙事教导我们，人类被造就带有对亲密关系的需求。创世记经文似乎默认，获得这种亲密体验的普遍途径是在婚姻圣约之内。虽然旧约叙事未曾明言，但似乎普遍将单身视为一种并非理想或令人向往的状态。

然而，在旧约圣经中，我们确实能够找到服事神学的萌芽，该神学充分肯定了单身人士独特的贡献。耶和华曾命令先知耶利米绝不可娶妻生子，以此作为向以色列人发出的审判预兆（耶利米书 16:1-4）。以西结和何西阿的婚姻也因着各自所领受的先知使命而历经中断与波折。

路得的故事虽以婚姻圆满落幕，但她在单身期间与寡妇拿俄米之间的关系，却以圣约般的忠诚（covenant loyalty）为标志，这种忠诚常被用作勉励即将步入婚姻之新人的典范。路得立誓跟随拿俄米同行，认同她的同胞，敬拜她的上帝，向拿俄米宣告了生死相随、不可分割的爱。

到了新约时代，耶稣与保罗皆是以单身身份开展事工。尽管圣经对耶稣的独身未作专门的教义性论述，但保罗在哥林多前书极力鼓励哥林多教会将独身视为上帝所赐的恩赐（哥林多前书 7:1-9, 25-35）。单身的好处包括免于配偶和儿女所带来的分心与挂虑。在艰难动荡的岁月中，单身人士无需时刻为家人的安危牵肠挂肚。保罗甚至认为单身优于婚姻——至少对于那些领受了独身恩赐的人而言确是如此。

教会蒙召当承认单身状态应得的尊荣，甚至在某些情况下比婚姻更为可取。教会必须谨慎留意，切不可将单身人士排斥在教会生活或事工之外，而当格外关切并切实接纳那些独身生活的人。

对亲密关系的需求给单身人士带来了独特的挑战。在性方面的圣洁贞守是圣经对单身者的明确吩咐（哥林多前书 7:8-9）。然而，创造叙事中所确认的人对伴侣关系的需求，在独身者身上并未消失。对于单身人士而言，建立健康而纯洁的人际关系，是在社会中活出丰盛、充实生命所不可或缺的。

Singleness

The creation narrative teaches that humans are created with the need for intimacy. The Genesis text appears to assume that the common pattern for finding intimacy will be within the marriage covenant. Although it is not specifically stated, the Old Testament narratives seem to assume that singleness is not a desirable state.

Within the Old Testament, however, we do find the roots of a theology of service that recognizes the unique contributions of single persons. Yahweh commands that Jeremiah the prophet never marry, as a sign to Israel ( Jeremiah 16:1-4). The marriages of Ezekiel and Hosea face interruptions related to their mission.

The story of Ruth results in marriage, but her relationship as a single person with the widow Naomi is characterized by covenant loyalty which is often cited as an example for a couple about to be married. Ruth pledges to go with Naomi, to join her people, and to worship her God. She declares an inseparable love for Naomi.

In the New Testament, both Jesus and Paul minister as single persons. Although Scripture is silent regarding Jesus’ celibacy, Paul encourages the church at Corinth to consider celibacy as a gift from God (1 Corinthians 7:1-9, 25-35). The benefits of singleness include freedom from distractions that a spouse and children create. Single persons in difficult times are not preoccupied with the security of their family members. Paul considers singleness to be superior to marriage, at least for those who have the gift of celibacy.

The church is called to recognize the honour due, even preference for, the single state. The church must take care not to exclude single persons from church life or from ministry. Special concern must be given to include those who live as singles.

The need for intimacy presents unique challenges to the single person. Sexual chastity is the biblical mandate for single persons (1 Corinthians 7:8-9). The need for partnership recognized in the creation story, however, is not absent in the celibate person. Healthy and pure relationships for single persons are necessary for living fulfilling lives in society.

家庭 (Family)

旧约中的家庭

家庭是上帝所设立、旨在抚育儿女的社会结构。以色列的核心信仰宣言（申命记 6:4-9）教导我们，属灵教导与品格塑造在日常家庭生活中施行最为有效。旧约发展出三大主题，宣告了上帝对家庭的心意：

  1. 儿女被视为上帝赐予家庭的礼物：儿女是上帝的祝福（诗篇 127:5）。撒拉生以撒、利百加生雅各与以扫、拉结生约瑟，以及哈拿生撒母耳的故事，构成了以色列子民历史的核心篇章。
  2. 诫命吩咐儿女要孝敬和尊重父母：儿女不仅蒙召“当孝敬父母”（出埃及记 20:12），而且咒骂或殴打父母在律法中属于极严重的死罪（出埃及记 21:17）。
  3. 圣约子民的福祉当通过父母的教导与引导世代相传：申命记 6:1-25 强调，父母的教训当包括述说上帝从埃及拯救以色列人的历史叙事、恪守以色列的节期与圣日，并在日常生活中随处谈论这些真理。

申命记 6:1-25 突出了四个要点：

  • 首先，当儿女聆听上帝伟大的救赎作为并顺服上帝的诫命时，便能得享福乐与长久的日子（6:1-3）。
  • 其次，与耶和华立约的核心乃是：“你要尽心、尽性、尽力爱耶和华你的神”（6:5）。
  • 第三，当以色列人进入应许之地时，对上帝的这份认识将保守他们不致离弃上帝去叩拜外邦假神（6:13-19）。
  • 第四，当历史距出埃及事件日久，儿女询问这些法度、律例和典章的意义时，父母必须清晰明确地作出解答（6:20-25）。

新约中的家庭

虽然耶稣为父母带到祂面前的小孩子祝福（马可福音 10:13-16），但耶稣也明确指出，在上帝的国度中，肉身的家庭纽带必须顺服于遵行上帝旨意的崇高呼召（马可福音 3:31-35；路加福音 14:26）。在真实的意义上，因着信靠耶稣基督而涌现出的新兴信仰共同体，本身就是信徒真正的属灵大家庭。

耶稣与祂肉身父母的关系，不仅体现出祂对父上帝及上帝之国的首要委身，同时也展现了祂对家庭的关切。在少年耶稣上圣殿的故事中（路加福音 2:41-52），经文记载耶稣同父母回到拿撒勒，并且顺从他们（2:51）。于是“耶稣的智慧和身量，并神和人喜爱他的心，都一齐增长”。当母亲马利亚在耶稣传道期间前来探视时，耶稣似乎在讲道授课结束之前推迟了与她的会面（马可福音 3:31-35）。然而在十字架上，耶稣极其深情地将马利亚托付给祂所爱的门徒照料（约翰福音 19:26-27）。

教会领袖当在健康家庭生活上树立榜样。在提摩太前书 3:1-13 所列出的领袖资格中，敬虔的领袖必须是那些能够证明自己善于管理家庭和儿女的人（3:4, 12）。

以弗所书 6:1-4 是新约圣经中论及儿女与父母关系最为权威、最具决定性的经文。使徒重申了第五条诫命（出埃及记 20:12），以提醒上帝的新约子民：儿女有责任恭敬、孝顺父母。在这条广为人知的诫命之上，使徒对父母提出了一条同等重要的吩咐：“你们作父亲的，不要惹儿女的气，只要照着主的教训和警戒养育他们”（6:4）。

Family

The family in the Old Testament: The family is the God-ordained social structure designed to nurture children. The primary confession of Israel in Deuteronomy 6:4-9 teaches that spiritual training best happens within day-to-day family life. The Old Testament develops three themes which declare God’s intent for families.

First, children are seen as a gift from God to families. Children are a blessing (Psalm 127:5). The stories of the births of Isaac to Sarah, Jacob and Esau to Rebekah, Joseph to Rachel, and Samuel to Hannah are central to the story of the people of Israel.

Second, children are commanded to honour and respect their parents. Not only were children called to “Honour your father and your mother” (Exodus 20:12), but it was also a capital offence to curse or to strike one’s father or mother (Exodus 21:17).

Third, the blessings of being a covenant people were to be perpetuated through parental instruction and guidance (Deuteronomy 6:1-25). This instruction was to include reciting the narrative of deliverance from Egypt, of keeping the feasts and holy days of Israel, and of speaking of these things each day.

Deuteronomy 6:1-25 highlights four distinct points: First, wellness results when children hear about the great salvation deeds of God and live a life of obedience to the commands of God (6:1-3). Second, the heart of the covenant with Yahweh is to “Love the LORD your God with all your heart and with all your soul and with all your strength” (6:5). Third, when the people of Israel would enter the promised land, this knowledge of God would keep them from turning to pagan gods (6:13-19). Fourth, when the people are a long time-period away from the exodus events and their children ask what these symbols and stories mean, they are to be answered clearly and unambiguously (6:20-25).

The family in the New Testament: Although Jesus blesses the children who were brought to him by their parents (Mark 10:13-16), Jesus makes it clear that in the kingdom of God, our family ties take second place to obeying the will of God (Mark 3:31-35; Luke 14:26). In a real sense, the new community of faith which emerges through belief in Jesus Christ is family for believers.

Jesus’ relationship with his parents reflects his primary commitment to God the Father and the reign of God, as well as his concern for family. In the story of young Jesus at the temple (Luke 2:41-52), it is recorded that Jesus went back to live with his parents in Nazareth and continued under their authority (2:51). Thus “Jesus grew in wisdom and in stature, and in favour with God and men.” When Mary comes to see her son during his ministry, Jesus seems to postpone contact with her until he has completed his teaching (Mark 3:31-35). At the cross, Jesus speaks to the beloved disciple about caring for Mary ( John 19:26-27).

Church leaders are to model healthy family life. Among the leadership qualifications mentioned in 1 Timothy 3:1-13, godly leaders are to be those who also demonstrate their ability to manage their families and households well (vv. 4, 12).

In Ephesians 6:1-4, we have the most definitive text of the New Testament on the subject of children and parents. The fifth commandment (Exodus 20:12) is repeated to remind the new people of God of the obligation of children to be courteous and respectful toward their parents. Added to this well-known epithet is an equally important command to parents: “... do not exasperate your children; instead, bring them up in the training and instruction of the Lord” (6:4).
